                frame_id = int(path.split("/")[-1])
                self._serve_frame_bin(frame_id)
            except ValueError:
                self.send_error(400, "Invalid frame ID")

        elif path.startswith("/api/frame/"):
            try:
                frame_id = int(path.split("/")[-1])
                self._serve_frame(frame_id)
            except ValueError:
                self._send_json({"error": "Invalid frame ID"}, 400)

        elif path == "/api/anomalies":
            self._serve_anomalies()

        elif path == "/api/sessions":
            self._serve_sessions()

        elif path == "/api/download":
            self._serve_download(parse_qs(parsed.query))

        else:
            self._send_json({"error": "Not Found"}, 404)

    def do_OPTIONS(self):
        self.send_response(204)
        self.send_header("Access-Control-Allow-Origin", "*")
        self.send_header("Access-Control-Allow-Methods", "GET, POST, OPTIONS")
        self.send_header("Access-Control-Allow-Headers", "Content-Type")
        self.end_headers()

    def do_POST(self):
        parsed = urlparse(self.path)
        path = parsed.path
        if path in ("/api/mcu/command", "/api/motor/command"):
            content_len = int(self.headers.get("Content-Length", 0))
            body = self.rfile.read(content_len) if content_len > 0 else b"{}"
            try:
                data = json.loads(body.decode("utf-8"))
            except Exception:
                self._send_json({"error": "Invalid JSON"}, 400)
                return

            action = data.get("action", "").lower()
            cmd_str = ""

            if action == "motors":
                enable = data.get("enable", True)
                cmd_str = "MOTORS\n" if enable else "DISABLE\n"
            elif action == "estop":
                cmd_str = "ESTOP\n"
            elif action == "pid":
                kp = float(data.get("kp", 0.1))
                ki = float(data.get("ki", 1.5))
                cmd_str = f"PID {kp:.3f} {ki:.3f}\n"
            elif action == "calib":
                axle = int(data.get("axle", 0))
                motor = int(data.get("motor", 1))
                voltage = float(data.get("voltage", 4.0))
                cmd_str = f"CALIB {axle} {motor} {voltage:.1f}\n"
            elif action == "save":
                cmd_str = "SAVE\n"
            elif action == "dfu":
                cmd_str = "DFU\n"
            elif action == "speed":
                v = float(data.get("v", 0.0))
                cmd_str = f"V {v:.2f}\n"
            elif action == "steer":
                deg = float(data.get("deg", 0.0))
                # Convert steering angle (-32.2..+32.2 deg) to servo dial angle (0..180 deg, 90=Center, 0=Left, 180=Right)
                dial_deg = max(0.0, min(180.0, 90.0 - (deg / 32.2166) * 90.0))
                cmd_str = f"A {dial_deg:.1f}\n"
            elif action == "trim":
                slider_trim = max(-60.0, min(60.0, float(data.get("trim_us", 0.0))))
                with STATE.lock:
                    center_us = STATE.servo_center_us
                total_trim_us = max(-500.0, min(500.0, (center_us - BASE_SERVO_NEUTRAL_US) + slider_trim))
                cmd_str = f"TRIM {total_trim_us:.1f}\n"
            elif action == "save_trim_center":
                slider_trim = max(-60.0, min(60.0, float(data.get("trim_us", 0.0))))
                new_center = STATE.save_servo_center(STATE.servo_center_us + slider_trim)
                total_trim_us = max(-500.0, min(500.0, new_center - BASE_SERVO_NEUTRAL_US))
                cmd_str = f"TRIM {total_trim_us:.1f}\n"
            elif action == "pulse":
                pulse = float(data.get("pulse", BASE_SERVO_NEUTRAL_US))
                cmd_str = f"P {pulse:.0f}\n"
            elif action == "bench":
                if "enabled" in data:
                    STATE.fake_data_active = bool(data["enabled"])
                else:
                    STATE.fake_data_active = not STATE.fake_data_active
                cmd_str = "BENCH\n"
            elif action == "reset":
                cmd_str = "RESET\n"
            elif action in ("reconnect", "reset_ports"):
                cmd_str = "RECONNECT\n"
            elif action == "status":
                cmd_str = "STATUS\n"
            elif action == "planner":
                enable = bool(data.get("enable", False))
                cmd_str = "START\n" if enable else "STOP\n"
            elif action == "auto_servo_center":
                enable = bool(data.get("enable", True))
                if enable:
                    res = STATE.auto_servo_center.start(
                        max_iterations=data.get("max_iterations"),
                        cruise_speed=data.get("cruise_speed"),
                        drive_distance_m=data.get("drive_distance_m"),
                        tolerance_deg_per_m=data.get("tolerance_deg_per_m"),
                        trim_offset_us=data.get("trim_offset_us"),
                    )
                    self._send_json({
                        "status": "started" if res.get("started") else "error",
                        "error": res.get("error"),
                        "action": action,
                        "servo_center_us": STATE.servo_center_us,
                        "auto_servo_center": STATE.auto_servo_center.get_status(),
                    }, 200 if res.get("started") else 409)
                else:
                    STATE.auto_servo_center.stop()
                    self._send_json({
                        "status": "stopped",
                        "action": action,
                        "servo_center_us": STATE.servo_center_us,
                        "auto_servo_center": STATE.auto_servo_center.get_status(),
                    })
                return
            elif action == "auto_servo_center_stop":
                STATE.auto_servo_center.stop()
                self._send_json({
                    "status": "stopped",
                    "action": action,
                    "servo_center_us": STATE.servo_center_us,
                    "auto_servo_center": STATE.auto_servo_center.get_status(),
                })
                return
            elif action == "keepalive":
                cmd_str = "CLI_KEEPALIVE\n"
            elif action == "raw":
                cmd_str = f"{data.get('cmd', '').strip()}\n"
            else:
                self._send_json({"error": f"Unknown action: {action}"}, 400)
                return

            if action in ("estop", "reset", "speed", "steer", "pulse", "bench") or (action == "motors" and not data.get("enable", True)) or (action == "planner" and data.get("enable", False)):
                if STATE.auto_servo_center.is_running():
                    STATE.auto_servo_center.stop(f"Aborted due to {action}")

            planner_active = False
            if action == "planner":
                enable = bool(data.get("enable", False))
                with STATE.lock:
                    planner_active = (time.time() - STATE.last_planner_pkt_time <= 1.0)
                ok_planner = send_planner_cmd(cmd_str.encode("utf-8"))
                if enable:
                    ok_mcu = send_mcu_cmd(b"E\n") and send_mcu_cmd(b"MOTORS\n")
                elif not planner_active:
                    ok_mcu = send_mcu_cmd(b"ESTOP\n") and send_mcu_cmd(b"RESET\n")
                else:
                    # Path planner handles 2.0s active braking before disabling motor drivers
                    ok_mcu = True
                ok = ok_planner and ok_mcu
            elif action == "save_trim_center":
                ok = send_mcu_cmd(cmd_str.encode("utf-8")) and send_mcu_cmd(b"TRIM_SAVE\n") and send_mcu_cmd(b"TRIM_GET\n")
            else:
                ok = send_mcu_cmd(cmd_str.encode("utf-8"))

            # Update cached state
            with STATE.lock:
                if action == "speed":
                    STATE.latest_pkt["v_cmd"] = float(data.get("v", 0.0))
                elif action == "steer":
                    STATE.latest_pkt["steer_cmd_deg"] = float(data.get("deg", 0.0))
                elif action == "estop":
                    STATE.latest_pkt["v_cmd"] = 0.0
                    STATE.latest_pkt["mode"] = "ESTOP"
                elif action == "motors":
                    STATE.latest_pkt["mode"] = "DRIVE" if data.get("enable", True) else "IDLE"
                elif action == "planner":
                    enable = bool(data.get("enable", False))
                    STATE.planner_running = enable
                    STATE.latest_pkt["planner_running"] = enable
                    if "flags" not in STATE.latest_pkt or not isinstance(STATE.latest_pkt["flags"], dict):
                        STATE.latest_pkt["flags"] = {}
                    STATE.latest_pkt["flags"]["planner_running"] = enable
                    STATE.latest_pkt["mode"] = "DRIVE" if enable else ("BRAKING" if planner_active else "STOPPED")
                    if not enable:
                        STATE.latest_pkt["v_cmd"] = 0.0
                        STATE.latest_pkt["steer_cmd_deg"] = 0.0
                        STATE.latest_pkt["best_line"] = []
                        STATE.latest_pkt["candidate_lines"] = []
                        STATE.latest_pkt["guide_line"] = []
                    STATE.invalidate_caches()
                elif action == "bench":
                    if "flags" not in STATE.latest_pkt:
                        STATE.latest_pkt["flags"] = {}
                    STATE.latest_pkt["flags"]["fake_data"] = STATE.fake_data_active
                    STATE.invalidate_caches()
                if action != "keepalive":
                    STATE.latest_pkt["last_action"] = action
                    STATE.latest_pkt["last_cmd_sent"] = cmd_str.strip()

            self._send_json({
                "status": "dispatched" if ok else "failed",
                "action": action,
                "cmd": cmd_str.strip(),
                "fake_data": STATE.fake_data_active,
                "planner_running": STATE.planner_running,
                "servo_center_us": STATE.servo_center_us,
                "pipe": "pi4 -> esp32 -> stm32"
            })
        else:
            self._send_json({"error": "Endpoint not found"}, 404)

    def _handle_websocket_upgrade(self, ws_key: str):
        guid = "258EAFA5-E914-47DA-95CA-C5AB0DC85B11"
        accept = base64.b64encode(hashlib.sha1((ws_key.strip() + guid).encode("utf-8")).digest()).decode("utf-8")
        handshake = (
            "HTTP/1.1 101 Switching Protocols\r\n"
            "Upgrade: websocket\r\n"
            "Connection: Upgrade\r\n"
            f"Sec-WebSocket-Accept: {accept}\r\n\r\n"
        )
        sock = self.connection
        sock.setsockopt(socket.IPPROTO_TCP, socket.TCP_NODELAY, 1)
        sock.settimeout(0.2)
        sock.sendall(handshake.encode("utf-8"))

        STATE.register_ws_client(sock)
        # Push latest frame immediately on connect
        with STATE.lock:
            cur_bin = STATE.get_cached_bin()
        if cur_bin:
            STATE.broadcast_ws_binary(cur_bin)

        try:
            while True:
                try:
                    data = sock.recv(1024)
                    if not data:
                        break
                    # Close frame
                    if (data[0] & 0x0F) == 0x08:
                        break
                    # Ping frame -> reply pong
                    elif (data[0] & 0x0F) == 0x09:
                        pong = bytearray(data)
                        pong[0] = (pong[0] & 0xF0) | 0x0A
                        sock.sendall(pong)
                except (socket.timeout, TimeoutError):
                    # Telemetry clients stream downlink; lack of uplink frames is normal
                    continue
        except Exception:
            pass
        finally:
            STATE.unregister_ws_client(sock)
            try:
                sock.close()
            except Exception:
                pass

    def _serve_file(self, filepath: str, content_type: str):
        if not os.path.exists(filepath):
            self.send_error(404, "File not found")
            return
        try:
            with open(filepath, "rb") as f:
                content = f.read()
            self.send_response(200)
            self.send_header("Content-Type", content_type)
            self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(content)))
            self.send_header("Connection", "keep-alive")
            self.send_header("Cache-Control", "no-cache")
            self.end_headers()
            self.wfile.write(content)
        except Exception as e:
            self.send_error(500, f"Error reading file: {e}")

    def _send_json(self, data: Any, code: int = 200):
        body = json.dumps(data).encode("utf-8")
        self.send_response(code)
        self.send_header("Content-Type", "application/json")
        self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(body)))
        self.send_header("Connection", "keep-alive")
        self.send_header("Access-Control-Allow-Origin", "*")
        self.end_headers()
        self.wfile.write(body)

    def _serve_status(self):
        stats = get_system_cpu_stats()
        now = time.time()
        if (now - STATE._last_disk_check) >= 5.0:
            STATE._last_disk_check = now
            try:
                disk_total, disk_used, disk_free = shutil.disk_usage(STATE.recorder.output_dir if STATE.recorder else CURR_DIR)
                STATE._cached_disk_free = round(disk_free / (1024**3), 2)
            except Exception:
                pass
            if STATE.recorder and os.path.exists(STATE.recorder.db_path):
                try:
                    STATE._cached_db_size = round(os.path.getsize(STATE.recorder.db_path) / (1024 * 1024), 2)
                except Exception:
                    pass

        data = {
            "session_id": STATE.recorder.session_id if STATE.recorder else "none",
            "min_frame_id": getattr(STATE.recorder, "min_frame_id", 1) if STATE.recorder else 1,
            "total_recorded": STATE.recorder.total_frames_recorded if STATE.recorder else 0,
            "dropped_frames": STATE.recorder.dropped_frames if STATE.recorder else 0,
            "pruned_frames": STATE.recorder.pruned_frames_count if STATE.recorder else 0,
            "driving_seconds": round(STATE.recorder.total_driving_seconds, 1) if STATE.recorder else 0.0,
            "driving_minutes": round(STATE.recorder.total_driving_seconds / 60.0, 1) if STATE.recorder else 0.0,
            "max_buffer_minutes": round((STATE.recorder.max_driving_seconds / 60.0), 1) if STATE.recorder else 30.0,
            "file_size_mb": STATE._cached_db_size,
            "disk_free_gb": STATE._cached_disk_free,
            "temp_c": stats.get("temp_c", 0.0),
            "loop_hz": round(STATE.loop_hz, 1),
            "cpu_affinity": list(get_core_affinity()),
            "servo_center_us": STATE.servo_center_us,
            "esp_servo_center_us": STATE.esp_servo_center_us,
            "auto_servo_center": STATE.auto_servo_center.get_status(),
        }
        self._send_json(data)

    def _serve_live(self):
        with STATE.lock:
            body = STATE.get_cached_json()
        self.send_response(200)
        self.send_header("Content-Type", "application/json")
        self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(body)))
        self.send_header("Connection", "keep-alive")
        self.send_header("Access-Control-Allow-Origin", "*")
        self.end_headers()
        self.wfile.write(body)

    def _serve_live_bin(self):
        with STATE.lock:
            body = STATE.get_cached_bin()
        self.send_response(200)
        self.send_header("Content-Type", "application/octet-stream")
        self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(body)))
        self.send_header("Connection", "keep-alive")
        self.send_header("Access-Control-Allow-Origin", "*")
        self.end_headers()
        self.wfile.write(body)

    def _serve_frame(self, frame_id: int):
        if not STATE.recorder:
            self._send_json({"error": "No active recorder"}, 500)
            return

        reader = TelemetryLogReader(STATE.recorder.db_path)
        frame_data = reader.get_frame(frame_id)
        if frame_data:
            self._send_json(frame_data)
        else:
            self._send_json({"error": f"Frame {frame_id} not found"}, 404)

    def _serve_frame_bin(self, frame_id: int):
        if not STATE.recorder:
            self.send_error(500, "No active recorder")
            return

        reader = TelemetryLogReader(STATE.recorder.db_path)
        frame_data = reader.get_frame(frame_id)
        if frame_data:
            try:
                body = build_live_binary_packet(frame_data)
            except Exception as e:
                self.send_error(500, f"Failed to pack frame {frame_id}: {e}")
                return
            self.send_response(200)
            self.send_header("Content-Type", "application/octet-stream")
            self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(body)))
            self.send_header("Access-Control-Allow-Origin", "*")
            self.end_headers()
            self.wfile.write(body)
        else:
            self.send_error(404, f"Frame {frame_id} not found")

    def _serve_anomalies(self):
        if not STATE.recorder:
            self._send_json([])
            return
        reader = TelemetryLogReader(STATE.recorder.db_path)
        anoms = reader.get_anomalies()
        self._send_json(anoms)

    def _serve_sessions(self):
        if not STATE.recorder or not os.path.isdir(STATE.recorder.output_dir):
            self._send_json([])
            return
        cur_name = os.path.basename(STATE.recorder.db_path)
        candidates = []
        for fname in os.listdir(STATE.recorder.output_dir):
            if not fname.endswith(".db"):
                continue
            fpath = os.path.join(STATE.recorder.output_dir, fname)
            if not os.path.isfile(fpath):
                continue
            try:
                st = os.stat(fpath)
                wal_path = fpath + "-wal"
                wal_size = os.path.getsize(wal_path) if os.path.exists(wal_path) else 0
                candidates.append((st.st_mtime, st.st_size + wal_size, fname, fpath))
            except Exception:
                pass
        candidates.sort(key=lambda item: item[0], reverse=True)

        sessions = []
        for mtime, total_size, fname, fpath in candidates[:12]:
            frames = 0
            try:
                reader = TelemetryLogReader(fpath)
                _, _, frames = reader.get_frame_bounds()
                reader.close()
            except Exception:
                pass
            sessions.append({
                "filename": fname,
                "size_bytes": total_size,
                "mtime": mtime,
                "frames": int(frames or 0),
                "is_current": fname == cur_name,
            })
        self._send_json(sessions)

    def _serve_download(self, query: Optional[Dict[str, List[str]]] = None):
        if not STATE.recorder:
            self.send_error(404, "Recorder not initialized")
            return

        target_path = STATE.recorder.db_path
        if query and "file" in query and query["file"]:
            req_name = os.path.basename(query["file"][0])
            if not req_name.endswith(".db"):
                self.send_error(400, "Invalid session filename")
                return
            target_path = os.path.join(STATE.recorder.output_dir, req_name)

        if not os.path.exists(target_path):
            self.send_error(404, "Recording not found")
            return

        filename = os.path.basename(target_path)
        tmp_path = None
        try:
            # Use sqlite3.backup() so frames still buffered in the WAL (-wal)
            # are merged into a consistent, self-contained .db snapshot.
            fd, tmp_path = tempfile.mkstemp(suffix=".db", prefix="dl_")
            os.close(fd)
            src_conn = sqlite3.connect(target_path, timeout=10.0)
            dst_conn = sqlite3.connect(tmp_path)
            try:
                src_conn.backup(dst_conn)
            finally:
                dst_conn.close()
                src_conn.close()

            with open(tmp_path, "rb") as f:
                content = f.read()
            self.send_response(200)
            self.send_header("Content-Type", "application/octet-stream")
            self.send_header("Content-Disposition", f'attachment; filename="{filename}"')
            self.send_header("Content-Length", str(len(content)))
            self.end_headers()
            self.wfile.write(content)
        except Exception as e:
            self.send_error(500, f"Error sending file: {e}")
        finally:
            if tmp_path and os.path.exists(tmp_path):
                try:
                    os.remove(tmp_path)
                except OSError:
                    pass


def udp_listener_worker():
    """
    Dedicated UDP telemetry ingestion and high-speed laptop forwarder on Core 2.
    Receives packets from Core 1 Planner, records to SQLite WAL flight recorder,
    and forwards full-fidelity 1cm telemetry directly to laptop workstation.
    """
    sock = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_DGRAM)
    sock.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_REUSEADDR, 1)
    # 2 MB socket receive buffer prevents kernel drops during peak burst traffic
    try:
        sock.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_RCVBUF, 2097152)
    except Exception:
        pass

    try:
        sock.bind((HOST_BIND, PORT_UDP_RECV))
        print(f"[DebugServer] UDP Telemetry Listener active on {HOST_BIND}:{PORT_UDP_RECV}")
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"[DebugServer FATAL] Failed to bind UDP {HOST_BIND}:{PORT_UDP_RECV}: {e}")
        return

    sock.settimeout(1.0)

    # UDP Forwarder socket to laptop (broadcast + direct unicast)
    fwd_sock = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_DGRAM)
    try:
        fwd_sock.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_BROADCAST, 1)
    except Exception:
        pass
    fwd_sock.setblocking(False)

    while True:
        try:
            data, _ = sock.recvfrom(65535)

            # Direct binary UDP forward to laptop (zero re-encoding or serialization overhead)
            with STATE.lock:
                targets = list(STATE.forward_targets)
            for target in targets:
                try:
                    fwd_sock.sendto(data, target)
                except Exception:
                    pass

            pkt = json.loads(data.decode("utf-8"))

            now = time.time()
            STATE.total_frames_received += 1
            STATE._hz_counter += 1

            if now - STATE._last_hz_calc >= 1.0:
                STATE.loop_hz = STATE._hz_counter / (now - STATE._last_hz_calc)
                STATE._hz_counter = 0
                STATE._last_hz_calc = now

            is_planner = ("best_line" in pkt) or ("plan_ms" in pkt) or ("corridor_left" in pkt)
            tof_diag_keys = (
                "tof_target0_mm",
                "tof_target1_mm",
                "tof_target0_status",
                "tof_target1_status",
            )
            planner_merge_keys = (
                "seq",
                "mcu_timestamp_us",
                "v_est",
                "rpms_cmd",
                "rpms_act",
                "flags",
                "mcu_msg",
                "mcu_log",
                "slip_state",
                "slip_ratio",
                "servo_pulse_us",
                "mcu_main_connected",
                "mcu_tof_connected",
                "mcu_main_port",
                "mcu_tof_port",
                "esp_servo_center_us",
                "esp_servo_trim_us",
            )
            has_attached_tof_diag = False

            with STATE.lock:
                if pkt.get("esp_servo_center_us") is not None:
                    try:
                        esp_c = float(pkt["esp_servo_center_us"])
                        if SERVO_PULSE_MIN_US <= esp_c <= SERVO_PULSE_MAX_US:
                            STATE.esp_servo_center_us = round(esp_c, 1)
                            if not STATE._has_saved_trim_file and not STATE.auto_servo_center.is_running():
                                STATE.servo_center_us = STATE.esp_servo_center_us
                    except Exception:
                        pass
                tof_diag_cache = getattr(STATE, "_latest_tof_diag", None)
                if tof_diag_cache is None:
                    tof_diag_cache = {}
                    STATE._latest_tof_diag = tof_diag_cache

                for tk in tof_diag_keys:
                    if tk in pkt:
                        tof_diag_cache[tk] = pkt[tk]

                planner_active = (now - STATE.last_planner_pkt_time <= 1.0)
                if is_planner:
                    if tof_diag_cache:
                        for tk, tv in tof_diag_cache.items():
                            if tk not in pkt:
                                pkt[tk] = tv
                                has_attached_tof_diag = True
                    STATE.latest_pkt = dict(pkt)
                    STATE.last_planner_pkt_time = now
                    planner_active = True
                elif not planner_active:
                    # Planner is offline: show direct MCU / bridge telemetry
                    STATE.latest_pkt = dict(pkt)
                else:
                    # Planner is active: copy before merging non-ToF live chassis/link status fields
                    # so queued recorder dicts are never mutated in-place.
                    updated = dict(STATE.latest_pkt)
                    for k in planner_merge_keys:
                        if k in pkt:
                            updated[k] = pkt[k]
                    STATE.latest_pkt = updated

                STATE.last_pkt_time = now
                STATE.invalidate_caches()
                bin_data = STATE.get_cached_bin() if is_planner else None

            # Push live binary frame to connected WebSockets (throttled to ~20 Hz to save Wi-Fi bandwidth;
            # full 60 Hz telemetry is still recorded locally on SD card for offline review)
            if bin_data and (now - getattr(STATE, "_last_ws_push", 0.0) >= 0.05):
                STATE._last_ws_push = now
                STATE.broadcast_ws_binary(bin_data)

            # Non-blocking write to Flight Recorder (skip idle STOPPED/STANDBY/OFFLINE frames when stationary)
            if STATE.recorder:
                eff_mode = str(pkt.get("mode") or STATE.latest_pkt.get("mode") or "").upper()
                v_cmd_val = abs(float(pkt.get("v_cmd", STATE.latest_pkt.get("v_cmd", 0.0))))
                v_real_val = abs(float(pkt.get("v_real", pkt.get("v_est", 0.0))))
                is_idle_stopped = (eff_mode in ("STOPPED", "STANDBY", "OFFLINE")) and v_cmd_val < 0.01 and v_real_val < 0.05
                if not is_idle_stopped and (is_planner or (not planner_active and (now - getattr(STATE, "_last_db_record", 0.0) >= 0.05))):
                    STATE._last_db_record = now
                    STATE.recorder.record_frame(pkt, raw_bytes=None if has_attached_tof_diag else data)

        except socket.timeout:
            continue
        except Exception as e:
            # Drop bad packets without crashing
            continue


def run_debug_server():
    """Main launcher for Debug Server & Flight Recorder on Core 2."""
    def _handle_sigterm(_signum, _frame):
        raise KeyboardInterrupt()

    signal.signal(signal.SIGTERM, _handle_sigterm)

    print("=" * 70)
    print("  FOLKRACE PI 4: REAL-TIME DEBUG SERVER & FLIGHT RECORDER")
    print("=" * 70)

    # 1. HARD HARDWARE ISOLATION: Pin strictly to Core 2 (or 2+3 for I/O)
    set_core_affinity({2, 3}, "DebugServer")

    # 2. Initialize Flight Recorder
    recordings_dir = os.path.join(CURR_DIR, "data", "recordings")
    STATE.recorder = TelemetryFlightRecorder(output_dir=recordings_dir)
    STATE.recorder.start()

    # 3. Start UDP Listener Thread on Core 2
    udp_thread = threading.Thread(target=udp_listener_worker, name="UDPListener", daemon=True)
    udp_thread.start()

    # 4. Start High-Performance Threading HTTP & WebSocket Server on Core 2
    server_address = (HOST_BIND, PORT_HTTP)
    httpd = ThreadingHTTPServer(server_address, DebugHTTPRequestHandler)
    print(f"[DebugServer] Web Mission Control GUI: http://localhost:{PORT_HTTP} or http://robot:{PORT_HTTP}")
    print(f"[DebugServer] Real-time Flight Black Box saving to: {STATE.recorder.db_path}")
    print("=" * 70)

    try:
        httpd.serve_forever()
    except KeyboardInterrupt:
        print("\n[DebugServer] Shutting down...")
    finally:
        if STATE.auto_servo_center.is_running():
            STATE.auto_servo_center.stop("DebugServer shutting down")
        send_mcu_cmd(b"ESTOP\n")
        httpd.server_close()
        if STATE.recorder:
            STATE.recorder.stop()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    import argparse
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Folkrace Debug Server")
    parser.add_argument("--port", type=int, default=PORT_HTTP, help="HTTP port")
    args = parser.parse_args()
    PORT_HTTP = args.port
    run_debug_server()



=== FILE: 2_pi4/fan_control.py ===
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
fan_control.py
Hardware PWM Fan Controller for Raspberry Pi 4 (BCM2711 PWM0 on GPIO 12 / Pin 32).
Designed for 4-wire PWM coolers (e.g. Raspberry Pi 5 Active Cooler / THRML 30-AC).

Features:
- Exact 25 kHz hardware PWM (40,000 ns period via BCM2711 PWM0).
- Silent base speed: fan always spins at silent minimum (30% PWM) when cool (<= 45°C).
- Temperature filtering & deadband: filters sensor noise, prevents RPM hunting.
- Asymmetric slew-rate limiting:
    * Rapid ramp-up (up to +8%/sec normally, instant if >= 78°C) to prevent overheating.
    * Ultra-gentle ramp-down (max -1.5%/sec) to respect heatsink thermal inertia and
      eliminate annoying acoustic rev-up / rev-down cycles.
- Thermal throttle protection: reaches 100% full speed at >= 80°C.
"""

import os
import signal
import sys
import time
from pathlib import Path

# Hardware PWM Configuration (GPIO 12, PWM0)
PWM_CHIP_DIR = Path("/sys/class/pwm/pwmchip0")
PWM0_DIR = PWM_CHIP_DIR / "pwm0"
PERIOD_NS = 40000      # 40,000 ns = 25 kHz (standard 4-wire fan frequency)

# Temperature Range (°C)
TEMP_MIN = 45.0        # Temp below which fan stays at MIN_SPEED
TEMP_THROTTLE = 81.0   # Thermal throttle threshold where fan reaches 100%
TEMP_EMERGENCY = 81.0  # At or above 81°C, switch to emergency ramp-up rate

# Speed Limits (Duty Fraction 0.0 - 1.0)
MIN_SPEED = 0.40       # Minimum duty cycle (40% baseline airflow)
MAX_SPEED = 1.00       # Maximum duty cycle (100% cooling)

# Hysteresis & Slew Rate Controls
POLL_INTERVAL = 1.0                # Seconds between control loop iterations
TEMP_FILTER_ALPHA = 0.10           # Heavy low-pass filter weight (10s time constant)
TEMP_DEADBAND = 1.0                # 1.0°C deadband: ignore tiny temp oscillations

RAMP_UP_RATE_PER_SEC = 0.04        # 4% duty cycle per second (regular ramp-up)
RAMP_UP_EMERGENCY_RATE = 0.08      # 8% duty cycle per second (emergency ramp-up at >= 81°C)
RAMP_DOWN_RATE_PER_SEC = 0.010     # 1% duty cycle per second (ramp-down)
DUTY_STEP_RESOLUTION = 0.005       # 0.5% duty cycle step quantization

THERMAL_ZONE = "/sys/class/thermal/thermal_zone0/temp"

def init_hardware_pwm() -> bool:
    # Wait for PWM chip directory to exist (up to 15s during early boot)
    for _ in range(30):
        if (PWM_CHIP_DIR / "export").exists():
            break
        time.sleep(0.5)

    if not (PWM_CHIP_DIR / "export").exists():
        print(f"[fan_control] Error: {PWM_CHIP_DIR}/export not found!")
        return False

    # Export pwm0 if not yet exported
    if not PWM0_DIR.exists():
        try:
            with open(PWM_CHIP_DIR / "export", "w") as f:
                f.write("0\n")
        except Exception:
            pass

    # Wait for sysfs files to appear after export
    for _ in range(20):
        if (PWM0_DIR / "period").exists() and (PWM0_DIR / "duty_cycle").exists():
            break
        time.sleep(0.2)

    # Configure period and enable with retries
    for attempt in range(5):
        try:
            # Disable first so period changes cleanly
            if (PWM0_DIR / "enable").exists():
                with open(PWM0_DIR / "enable", "w") as f:
                    f.write("0\n")
            # Duty cycle must be <= period, reset to 0 first
            with open(PWM0_DIR / "duty_cycle", "w") as f:
                f.write("0\n")
            with open(PWM0_DIR / "period", "w") as f:
                f.write(f"{PERIOD_NS}\n")
            with open(PWM0_DIR / "enable", "w") as f:
                f.write("1\n")

            # Verify period was set correctly
            read_period = int((PWM0_DIR / "period").read_text().strip())
            if read_period == PERIOD_NS:
                print(f"[fan_control] PWM0 initialized successfully ({PERIOD_NS} ns period)")
                return True
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[fan_control] Retrying PWM0 setup (attempt {attempt+1}/5): {e}")
            time.sleep(0.5)

    print("[fan_control] Failed to configure PWM0 period after 5 attempts")
    return False

def set_fan_duty(duty_fraction: float):
    duty_fraction = max(MIN_SPEED, min(MAX_SPEED, duty_fraction))
    duty_ns = int(round(duty_fraction * PERIOD_NS))
    try:
        with open(PWM0_DIR / "duty_cycle", "w") as f:
            f.write(f"{duty_ns}\n")
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"[fan_control] Error updating duty_cycle: {e}, reinitializing PWM...")
        if init_hardware_pwm():
            try:
                with open(PWM0_DIR / "duty_cycle", "w") as f:
                    f.write(f"{duty_ns}\n")
            except Exception:
                pass

def get_raw_cpu_temp() -> float:
    try:
        with open(THERMAL_ZONE, "r") as f:
            return float(f.read().strip()) / 1000.0
    except Exception:
        return 80.0  # Fallback to high temp for safety if read fails

def calculate_target_duty(temp: float) -> float:
    if temp <= TEMP_MIN:
        return MIN_SPEED
    if temp >= TEMP_THROTTLE:
        return MAX_SPEED

    # Progressive curve (power 1.35 keeps fan extra quiet in 45-65°C range)
    fraction = (temp - TEMP_MIN) / (TEMP_THROTTLE - TEMP_MIN)
    speed = MIN_SPEED + (MAX_SPEED - MIN_SPEED) * (fraction ** 1.35)
    return max(MIN_SPEED, min(MAX_SPEED, speed))

def main():
    print(f"[fan_control] Starting 25 kHz Hardware PWM0 Controller on GPIO 12 (Pin 32)")
    print(f"[fan_control] Baseline: {int(MIN_SPEED*100)}% (<= {TEMP_MIN}°C) -> 100% (>= {TEMP_THROTTLE}°C)")
    print(f"[fan_control] Hysteresis: deadband {TEMP_DEADBAND}°C (LPF alpha: {TEMP_FILTER_ALPHA}) | ramp-up +{int(RAMP_UP_RATE_PER_SEC*100)}%/s (emergency: +{int(RAMP_UP_EMERGENCY_RATE*100)}%/s @ >={TEMP_EMERGENCY}°C) | ramp-down -{RAMP_DOWN_RATE_PER_SEC*100:.1f}%/s")

    init_hardware_pwm()

    def handle_exit(signum, frame):
        print("[fan_control] Shutting down, leaving fan at safe 50%...")
        set_fan_duty(0.50)
        sys.exit(0)

    signal.signal(signal.SIGINT, handle_exit)
    signal.signal(signal.SIGTERM, handle_exit)

    # Initial start spin-up kick (0.5s at 100% to overcome static friction)
    set_fan_duty(1.0)
    time.sleep(0.5)

    raw_temp = get_raw_cpu_temp()
    filtered_temp = raw_temp
    last_eval_temp = filtered_temp
    current_duty = calculate_target_duty(filtered_temp)
    set_fan_duty(current_duty)

    last_loop_time = time.time()
    last_logged_time = 0.0
    last_logged_duty = current_duty

    print(f"[fan_control] Running. Initial CPU temp: {filtered_temp:.1f}°C, Duty: {int(current_duty*100)}%")

    while True:
        time.sleep(POLL_INTERVAL)
        now = time.time()
        dt = max(0.1, min(2.0, now - last_loop_time))
        last_loop_time = now

        raw_temp = get_raw_cpu_temp()
        filtered_temp = (TEMP_FILTER_ALPHA * raw_temp) + ((1.0 - TEMP_FILTER_ALPHA) * filtered_temp)

        # Apply temperature deadband: only update target temperature if change exceeds deadband
        if abs(filtered_temp - last_eval_temp) >= TEMP_DEADBAND:
            last_eval_temp = filtered_temp

        target_duty = calculate_target_duty(last_eval_temp)

        # Asymmetric Slew-Rate Limiting (Ramp Up vs Ramp Down)
        if target_duty > current_duty:
            if filtered_temp >= TEMP_EMERGENCY:
                # Emergency ramp-up at >= 80°C (10%/s)
                max_rise = RAMP_UP_EMERGENCY_RATE * dt
            else:
                # Regular ramp-up (5%/s)
                max_rise = RAMP_UP_RATE_PER_SEC * dt
            current_duty = min(target_duty, current_duty + max_rise)
        elif target_duty < current_duty:
            # Gentle, silent ramp-down respecting heatsink thermal storage
            max_fall = RAMP_DOWN_RATE_PER_SEC * dt
            current_duty = max(target_duty, current_duty - max_fall)

        # Quantize step to prevent micro-fluctuations in sound pitch
        quantized_duty = round(current_duty / DUTY_STEP_RESOLUTION) * DUTY_STEP_RESOLUTION
        quantized_duty = max(MIN_SPEED, min(MAX_SPEED, quantized_duty))
        set_fan_duty(quantized_duty)

        # Periodic or event-driven logging
        if abs(quantized_duty - last_logged_duty) >= 0.04 or (now - last_logged_time) >= 60.0:
            print(f"[fan_control] CPU: {raw_temp:.1f}°C (filt: {filtered_temp:.1f}°C) | Fan: {int(quantized_duty*100)}% ({int(quantized_duty * PERIOD_NS)} ns)")
            last_logged_duty = quantized_duty
            last_logged_time = now

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()


=== FILE: 2_pi4/mcu_telemetry_protocol.py ===
"""
================================================================================
  FOLKRACE PI 4: MCU BINARY TELEMETRY PROTOCOL DECODER & ENCODER
================================================================================
  High-speed zero-copy Python bridge matching brain/mcu_telemetry_protocol.h:
    - Chassis Dynamics Packet (ESP32 Main -> Pi 4): 54 Bytes
    - ToF Perception Packet (ESP32 ToF Hub -> Pi 4): 74 Bytes
    - Motion Command Packet (Pi 4 -> ESP32 Main): 13 Bytes

  Decodes in < 1 microsecond per packet using native C Python struct unpack.
================================================================================
"""

import struct
from typing import Dict, Any, Optional, Tuple

# Sync Bytes
MCU_SYNC = b"\xAA\x55"
PI_SYNC = b"\x55\xAA"

MSG_ID_CHASSIS_TELEM = 0x01
MSG_ID_TOF_TELEM = 0x02
MSG_ID_TOF_SUB_TELEM = 0x05
MSG_ID_MOTION_CMD = 0x10

# Struct formats (little-endian '<', packed)
# Chassis: sync(2), msg_type(B), len(B), seq(I), ts(I),
#          IMU & Steer (8h: yaw, pitch, roll, gyro_z, ax, ay, steer_cmd, steer_act),
#          servo_us(H),
#          Speeds & RPMs (10h: v_cmd, v_est, fl_cmd, fr_cmd, rl_cmd, rr_cmd, fl_act, fr_act, rl_act, rr_act),
#          slip_state(B), slip_ratio(b), flags(B), chk(B)
CHASSIS_FORMAT = "<2sBBII8hH10hBbBB"
CHASSIS_SIZE = struct.calcsize(CHASSIS_FORMAT)  # 54 bytes

# ToF 16-zone full frame: sync(2), msg_type(B), sensor_id(B), seq(I), ts(I),
#      dist_mm(16H), status(16B), spad(16B), temp(b), chk(B)
TOF_FORMAT = "<2sBBII16H16B16BbB"
TOF_SIZE = struct.calcsize(TOF_FORMAT)  # 78 bytes

# ToF 4-zone per-sensor frame (ESP32_ToF/src/main.cpp ToF_Binary_Packet):
#      sync(2), sensor_id(B), seq(I), ts_ms(I), dist_mm(4H), chk(B)
TOF_SUB_FORMAT = "<2sBII4HB"
TOF_SUB_SIZE = struct.calcsize(TOF_SUB_FORMAT)  # 20 bytes

# ToF 44-byte multi-target 4-zone frame (ESP32_ToF/src/main.cpp ToF_Binary_Packet_44, no SPAD):
#      sync(2), sensor_id(B), seq(I), ts_ms(I), dist_mm(4H), target0_mm(4H), target1_mm(4H),
#      target0_status(4B), target1_status(4B), chk(B)
TOF_SUB44_FORMAT = "<2sBII4H4H4H4B4BB"
TOF_SUB44_SIZE = struct.calcsize(TOF_SUB44_FORMAT)  # 44 bytes

# ToF 52-byte multi-target 4-zone frame (ESP32_ToF/src/main.cpp ToF_Binary_Packet_52):
#      sync(2), sensor_id(B), seq(I), ts_ms(I), dist_mm(4H), target0_mm(4H), target1_mm(4H),
#      target0_status(4B), target1_status(4B), spad_count(4H), chk(B)
TOF_SUB52_FORMAT = "<2sBII4H4H4H4B4B4HB"
TOF_SUB52_SIZE = struct.calcsize(TOF_SUB52_FORMAT)  # 52 bytes

# ToF 60-byte per-sensor frame (ESP32_ToF/src/main.cpp multi-target ToF_Binary_Packet):
#      sync(2), sensor_id(B), seq(I), ts_ms(I), dist_mm(16H), status(16B), chk(B)
TOF_SUB60_FORMAT = "<2sBII16H16BB"
TOF_SUB60_SIZE = struct.calcsize(TOF_SUB60_FORMAT)  # 60 bytes

# Motion Command: sync(2), msg_type(B), seq(I), v_cmd(h), steer_mrad(h), mode(B), chk(B)
CMD_FORMAT = "<2sBIhhBB"
CMD_SIZE = struct.calcsize(CMD_FORMAT)  # 13 bytes


def calc_checksum(data: bytes) -> int:
    """Computes XOR checksum over bytes."""
    chk = 0
    for b in data:
        chk ^= b
    return chk


def decode_chassis_packet(data: bytes) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
    """Decodes 54-byte binary Chassis telemetry frame into a clean Python dictionary."""
    if len(data) < CHASSIS_SIZE:
        return None

    unpacked = struct.unpack_from(CHASSIS_FORMAT, data)
    sync, msg_type, payload_len, seq, ts_us = unpacked[0:5]

    if sync != MCU_SYNC or msg_type != MSG_ID_CHASSIS_TELEM or payload_len != (CHASSIS_SIZE - 5):
        return None

    expected_chk = unpacked[-1]
    actual_chk = calc_checksum(data[: CHASSIS_SIZE - 1])
    if actual_chk != expected_chk:
        return None  # Checksum mismatch

    (
        yaw_x10, pitch_x10, roll_x10, gyro_z_mrad, ax_cm, ay_cm,
        steer_cmd_x10, steer_act_x10, servo_us, v_cmd_mm, v_est_mm,
        fl_cmd, fr_cmd, rl_cmd, rr_cmd,
        fl_act, fr_act, rl_act, rr_act,
        slip_state_code, slip_ratio_pct, flags
    ) = unpacked[5:27]

    slip_map = {0: "NORMAL", 1: "UNDERSTEER", 2: "OVERSTEER", 3: "SPIN"}

    return {
        "seq": seq,
        "mcu_timestamp_us": ts_us,
        "yaw_deg": round(yaw_x10 / 10.0, 1),
        "pitch_deg": round(pitch_x10 / 10.0, 1),
        "roll_deg": round(roll_x10 / 10.0, 1),
        "gyro_z": round(gyro_z_mrad / 1000.0, 3),
        "accel_fwd": round(ax_cm / 100.0, 2),
        "accel_lat": round(ay_cm / 100.0, 2),
        "steer_cmd_deg": round(steer_cmd_x10 / 10.0, 1),
        "steer_actual_deg": round(steer_act_x10 / 10.0, 1),
        "servo_pulse_us": servo_us,
        "v_cmd": round(v_cmd_mm / 1000.0, 3),
        "v_real": round(v_est_mm / 1000.0, 3),
        "v_est": round(v_est_mm / 1000.0, 3),
        "rpms_cmd": [float(fl_cmd), float(fr_cmd), float(rl_cmd), float(rr_cmd)],
        "rpms_act": [float(fl_act), float(fr_act), float(rl_act), float(rr_act)],
        "slip_state": slip_map.get(slip_state_code, "NORMAL"),
        "slip_ratio": round(slip_ratio_pct / 100.0, 2),
        "flags": {
            "override_active": bool(flags & 0x01),
            "fake_data": bool(flags & 0x02),
            "imu_online": bool(flags & 0x04),
            "stm32_front": bool(flags & 0x08),
            "stm32_rear": bool(flags & 0x10),
        },
    }


def decode_tof_packet(data: bytes) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
    """Decodes 74-byte binary ToF telemetry frame."""
    if len(data) < TOF_SIZE:
        return None

    unpacked = struct.unpack_from(TOF_FORMAT, data)
    sync, msg_type, sensor_id, seq, ts_us = unpacked[0:5]

    if sync != MCU_SYNC or msg_type != MSG_ID_TOF_TELEM:
        return None

    expected_chk = unpacked[-1]
    actual_chk = calc_checksum(data[: TOF_SIZE - 1])
    if actual_chk != expected_chk:
        return None

    dist_mm = list(unpacked[5:21])
    status = list(unpacked[21:37])
    spad = list(unpacked[37:53])
    silicon_temp = unpacked[53]

    # Convert mm to meters (clamp every reading below 30mm to 30mm for Pi reporting)
    dist_mm = [max(30, d) if d < 30 else d for d in dist_mm]
    ranges_m = [round(d / 1000.0, 3) for d in dist_mm]

    return {
        "sensor_id": sensor_id,
        "seq": seq,
        "mcu_timestamp_us": ts_us,
        "silicon_temp_c": silicon_temp,
        "distances_m": ranges_m,
        "status": status,
        "spad": spad,
    }


VALID_TOF_STATUSES = {5, 6, 9}  # ST Table 4 valid statuses (Status 10 is treated as an error/invalid)


def _unpack_tof_sub_multi_target(data: bytes, fmt: str, has_spad: bool) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
    """Shared decoder for 44-byte and 52-byte multi-target 4-zone ToF packets."""
    size = len(data)
    unpacked = struct.unpack_from(fmt, data)
    sync, sensor_id, seq, ts_ms = unpacked[0:4]
    if sync != MCU_SYNC or not (1 <= sensor_id <= 4):
        return None

    if calc_checksum(data[: size - 1]) != unpacked[-1]:
        return None

    clamped_mm = [max(30, d) if d < 30 else d for d in unpacked[4:8]]
    result = {
        "sensor_id": sensor_id,
        "seq": seq,
        "ts_ms": ts_ms,
        "mcu_timestamp_us": ts_ms * 1000,
        "distances_m": [round(d / 1000.0, 3) for d in clamped_mm],
        "dist_mm": list(clamped_mm),
        "target0_mm": list(unpacked[8:12]),
        "target1_mm": list(unpacked[12:16]),
        "target0_status": list(unpacked[16:20]),
        "target1_status": list(unpacked[20:24]),
    }
    if has_spad:
        result["spad_count"] = list(unpacked[24:28])
    return result


def decode_tof_sub_packet(data: bytes) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
    """Decodes per-sensor binary ToF packet (20B lean, 44B multi-target, or 52B debug frame)."""
    # 1. Check exact 20-byte lean frame
    if len(data) == TOF_SUB_SIZE:
        unpacked = struct.unpack_from(TOF_SUB_FORMAT, data)
        sync, sensor_id, seq, ts_ms = unpacked[0:4]
        dist_mm = unpacked[4:8]

        if sync != MCU_SYNC or not (1 <= sensor_id <= 4):
            return None

        expected_chk = unpacked[8]
        actual_chk = calc_checksum(data[: TOF_SUB_SIZE - 1])
        if actual_chk != expected_chk:
            return None

        clamped_mm = [max(30, d) if d < 30 else d for d in dist_mm]
        return {
            "sensor_id": sensor_id,
            "seq": seq,
            "ts_ms": ts_ms,
            "mcu_timestamp_us": ts_ms * 1000,
            "distances_m": [round(d / 1000.0, 3) for d in clamped_mm],
            "dist_mm": list(clamped_mm),
        }

    # 2. Check exact 44-byte multi-target frame (Pi mode, no SPAD)
    if len(data) == TOF_SUB44_SIZE:
        return _unpack_tof_sub_multi_target(data, TOF_SUB44_FORMAT, has_spad=False)

    # 3. Check exact 52-byte multi-target frame (debug mode, with SPAD)
    if len(data) == TOF_SUB52_SIZE:
        return _unpack_tof_sub_multi_target(data, TOF_SUB52_FORMAT, has_spad=True)

    # 3. Check exact 60-byte legacy frame
    if len(data) == TOF_SUB60_SIZE:
        unpacked = struct.unpack_from(TOF_SUB60_FORMAT, data)
        sync, sensor_id, seq, ts_ms = unpacked[0:4]
        if sync != MCU_SYNC or not (1 <= sensor_id <= 4):
            return None
        expected_chk = unpacked[-1]
        actual_chk = calc_checksum(data[: TOF_SUB60_SIZE - 1])
        if actual_chk != expected_chk:
            return None

        dist_raw = unpacked[4:20]
        status_raw = unpacked[20:36]
        valid_statuses = VALID_TOF_STATUSES

        primary_distances_m = []
        for i in range(4):
            d0, d1 = dist_raw[2 * i], dist_raw[2 * i + 1]
            s0, s1 = status_raw[2 * i], status_raw[2 * i + 1]
            v0 = (s0 in valid_statuses and d0 > 0)
            v1 = (s1 in valid_statuses and d1 > 0)
            near0 = (s0 != 0 and s0 != 255 and 1 <= d0 <= 30)
            near1 = (s1 != 0 and s1 != 255 and 1 <= d1 <= 30)

            if v0 and v1:
                chosen = min(d0, d1)
            elif v0:
                chosen = d0
            elif v1:
                chosen = d1
            elif near0 and near1:
                chosen = min(d0, d1)
            elif near0:
                chosen = d0
            elif near1:
                chosen = d1
            else:
                chosen = 3000

            if chosen < 30:
                chosen = 30
            primary_distances_m.append(round(chosen / 1000.0, 3))

        return {
            "sensor_id": sensor_id,
            "seq": seq,
            "ts_ms": ts_ms,
            "mcu_timestamp_us": ts_ms * 1000,
            "distances_m": primary_distances_m,
            "distances_raw_mm": list(dist_raw),
            "status_raw": list(status_raw),
        }

    return None


def encode_motion_command(target_v_mps: float, target_steer_rad: float, run_mode: int = 1, seq: int = 0) -> bytes:
    """Encodes a 13-byte binary motion command packet for ESP32 Main."""
    v_mm_s = int(round(target_v_mps * 1000.0))
    steer_mrad = int(round(target_steer_rad * 1000.0))
    
    # Pack without checksum first
    header_payload = struct.pack("<2sBIhhB", PI_SYNC, MSG_ID_MOTION_CMD, seq, v_mm_s, steer_mrad, run_mode)
    chk = calc_checksum(header_payload)
    return header_payload + bytes([chk])


if __name__ == "__main__":
    import time
    print("Testing MCU Telemetry Protocol...")
    cmd = encode_motion_command(2.5, -0.35, 1, 42)
    assert len(cmd) == 13, f"Expected 13 bytes, got {len(cmd)}"
    assert calc_checksum(cmd[:-1]) == cmd[-1], "Checksum mismatch"
    print(f"Motion Command encoded: {len(cmd)} bytes -> OK")

    # Synthetic chassis packet
    payload = struct.pack(
        "<2sBBI" "I" "8h" "H" "10h" "BbBB",
        MCU_SYNC, MSG_ID_CHASSIS_TELEM, 49, 101,
        12345678,
        150, -20, 5, 250, 120, -15, 180, 175,
        1620,
        2500, 2480, 1200, 1220, 1200, 1220, 1195, 1215, 1202, 1225,
        0, 3, 0x1C, 0
    )
    chk = calc_checksum(payload[:-1])
    pkt = payload[:-1] + bytes([chk])
    
    t0 = time.perf_counter()
    res = decode_chassis_packet(pkt)
    dt_us = (time.perf_counter() - t0) * 1e6
    assert res is not None, "Failed to decode chassis packet"
    assert res["v_cmd"] == 2.5
    assert res["yaw_deg"] == 15.0
    print(f"Chassis Packet decoded in {dt_us:.2f} us -> OK: {res}")
    print("All MCU telemetry protocol tests passed!")



=== FILE: 2_pi4/mcu_uart_bridge.py ===
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
================================================================================
  FOLKRACE PI 4: HIGH-SPEED MCU BINARY UART INGESTION BRIDGE
================================================================================
  Listens to binary UART telemetry from:
    1. ESP32 Main (Chassis Dynamics, 4-Wheel RPMs, IMU, Servo Feedback) @ 100 Hz
    2. ESP32 ToF Hub (VL53L8CX 16-zone distance arrays) @ 60 Hz

  Feeds parsed real-hardware frames directly to:
    - Path Planner Pipeline (Core 1)
    - Debug Server & Black Box Flight Recorder (Core 2) via UDP 127.0.0.1:9876
================================================================================
"""

import os
import sys
import time
import signal
import socket
import select
import json
import glob
import threading
from typing import Optional, Dict, Any, Tuple, List, Set

CURR_DIR = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
if CURR_DIR not in sys.path:
    sys.path.insert(0, CURR_DIR)

from mcu_telemetry_protocol import (
    MCU_SYNC,
    PI_SYNC,
    MSG_ID_CHASSIS_TELEM,
    MSG_ID_TOF_TELEM,
    MSG_ID_TOF_SUB_TELEM,
    CHASSIS_SIZE,
    TOF_SIZE,
    TOF_SUB_SIZE,
    TOF_SUB44_SIZE,
    TOF_SUB52_SIZE,
    TOF_SUB60_SIZE,
    CMD_SIZE,
    decode_chassis_packet,
    decode_tof_packet,
    decode_tof_sub_packet,
    encode_motion_command,
)
from tof_latency_compensator import ToFLatencyCompensator, SENSOR_NAMES

# Mapping of sensor_id (1..4) from ESP32_ToF to 16-channel array slots matching SENSOR_NAMES:
# Sensor 1: Front-Left Outer (49.1° to 82.9° Left)  -> slots 4..7
# Sensor 2: Front-Left Center (4.1° to 37.9° Left)   -> slots 0..3
# Sensor 3: Front-Right Outer (49.1° to 82.9° Right) -> slots 12..15
# Sensor 4: Front-Right Center (4.1° to 37.9° Right) -> slots 8..11
SENSOR_CHANNEL_SLOTS = {
    1: [4, 5, 6, 7],
    2: [0, 1, 2, 3],
    3: [12, 13, 14, 15],
    4: [8, 9, 10, 11],
}
CRITICAL_TOF_SENSORS = (2, 4)  # Front-Left Center & Front-Right Center (critical for forward driving)

try:
    import serial
    HAS_SERIAL = True
except ImportError:
    HAS_SERIAL = False

UDP_DISPATCH_HOST = "127.0.0.1"
UDP_DISPATCH_PORT = 9876
UDP_CMD_PORT = 9877
UART_BAUD = 921600
LINK_TIMEOUT_S = 0.25          # 250ms link-loss failsafe (matches ESP32-STM32 250ms timeout)
RECONNECT_SILENCE_S = 1.2      # 1.2s total silence before tearing down & re-probing USB port
FRONT_TOF_REBOOT_S = 0.40      # 400ms front-ToF stall before auto-rebooting ESP32_ToF
TOF_BOOT_GRACE_S = 3.0         # 3.0s grace period after connect for VL53L8CX firmware upload
TOF_REBOOT_COOLDOWN_S = 5.0    # Minimum seconds between automatic ESP32_ToF reboots


class BinaryStreamParser:
    """Zero-allocation rolling stream parser for binary UART packets."""

    def __init__(self, is_tof_port: bool = False):
        self.is_tof_port = is_tof_port
        self.buffer = bytearray()

    def feed(self, chunk: bytes):
        self.buffer.extend(chunk)

    def extract_packets(self):
        """Yields (msg_type, decoded_dict) for all valid packets in buffer."""
        while len(self.buffer) >= 3:
            # 1. Search for sync header 0xAA 0x55
            idx = self.buffer.find(MCU_SYNC)
            if idx == -1:
                # Check for complete ASCII text lines from MCU (e.g. status or command responses)
                if not self.is_tof_port and b"\n" in self.buffer:
                    nl_idx = self.buffer.rfind(b"\n")
                    text_bytes = bytes(self.buffer[:nl_idx])
                    del self.buffer[:nl_idx + 1]
                    try:
                        text_str = text_bytes.decode("utf-8", errors="ignore")
                        for line in text_str.splitlines():
                            line = line.strip()
                            if line and len(line) >= 3 and all(c.isprintable() or c in "\r\n\t" for c in line):
                                yield ("TEXT", line)
                    except Exception:
                        pass
                else:
                    # Keep last byte in case header split across reads
                    self.buffer = self.buffer[-1:]
                break

            if idx > 0:
                if not self.is_tof_port:
                    prefix = bytes(self.buffer[:idx])
                    if b"\n" in prefix:
                        try:
                            text_str = prefix.decode("utf-8", errors="ignore")
                            for line in text_str.splitlines():
                                line = line.strip()
                                if line and len(line) >= 3 and all(c.isprintable() or c in "\r\n\t" for c in line):
                                    yield ("TEXT", line)
                        except Exception:
                            pass
                del self.buffer[:idx]

            if len(self.buffer) < 3:
                break

            msg_type_or_sensor_id = self.buffer[2]

            if not self.is_tof_port:
                # ESP32-Main Port
                if msg_type_or_sensor_id == MSG_ID_CHASSIS_TELEM:
                    if len(self.buffer) >= 4 and self.buffer[3] != (CHASSIS_SIZE - 5):
                        # False sync header inside payload; advance 2 bytes to rescan
                        del self.buffer[:2]
                        continue
                    if len(self.buffer) < CHASSIS_SIZE:
                        break
                    raw_pkt = bytes(self.buffer[:CHASSIS_SIZE])
                    decoded = decode_chassis_packet(raw_pkt)
                    if decoded:
                        del self.buffer[:CHASSIS_SIZE]
                        yield (MSG_ID_CHASSIS_TELEM, decoded)
                    else:
                        # Checksum mismatch on false sync; advance 2 bytes so nested header isn't lost
                        del self.buffer[:2]
                else:
                    del self.buffer[:2]
            else:
                # ESP32-ToF Port (supports 44B multi-target Pi frame, 52B debug frame, 20B lean frame, and 78B full array)
                if 1 <= msg_type_or_sensor_id <= 4:
                    matched_pkt = None
                    for pkt_size in (TOF_SUB44_SIZE, TOF_SUB52_SIZE, TOF_SUB_SIZE):
                        if len(self.buffer) >= pkt_size:
                            chk_calc = 0
                            for b in self.buffer[: pkt_size - 1]:
                                chk_calc ^= b
                            if chk_calc == self.buffer[pkt_size - 1]:
                                raw_pkt = bytes(self.buffer[:pkt_size])
                                decoded = decode_tof_sub_packet(raw_pkt)
                                if decoded:
                                    del self.buffer[:pkt_size]
                                    matched_pkt = decoded
                                    break

                    if matched_pkt is not None:
                        yield (MSG_ID_TOF_SUB_TELEM, matched_pkt)
                        continue

                    if len(self.buffer) >= TOF_SUB52_SIZE:
                        del self.buffer[:2]
                        continue
                    else:
                        break
                elif msg_type_or_sensor_id == MSG_ID_TOF_TELEM:
                    if len(self.buffer) < TOF_SIZE:
                        break
                    raw_pkt = bytes(self.buffer[:TOF_SIZE])
                    del self.buffer[:TOF_SIZE]
                    decoded = decode_tof_packet(raw_pkt)
                    if decoded:
                        yield (MSG_ID_TOF_TELEM, decoded)
                else:
                    del self.buffer[:2]


def open_low_latency_serial(port: str, baud: int = UART_BAUD) -> Any:
    """
    Opens a serial port with non-blocking raw I/O, clears HUPCL so closing/reopening
    never drops DTR/RTS to reset the ESP32, and enables kernel ASYNC_LOW_LATENCY.
    """
    ser = serial.Serial(port, baud, timeout=0.0, write_timeout=0.0)

    # Configure Linux termios (~HUPCL + VMIN=0/VTIME=0) and kernel low-latency flag
    if sys.platform == "linux":
        try:
            import fcntl
            import termios
            import array
            fd = ser.fileno()
            attrs = termios.tcgetattr(fd)
            # Clear HUPCL so closing the port never pulses DTR/RTS to reset the ESP32
            attrs[2] &= ~termios.HUPCL
            # Raw VMIN=0, VTIME=0 for instant non-blocking os.read()
            attrs[6][termios.VMIN] = 0
            attrs[6][termios.VTIME] = 0
            termios.tcsetattr(fd, termios.TCSANOW, attrs)
            try:
                # TIOCGSERIAL = 0x541E, TIOCSSERIAL = 0x541F, ASYNC_LOW_LATENCY = 0x2000
                buf = array.array("i", [0] * 32)
                fcntl.ioctl(fd, 0x541E, buf, True)
                buf[4] |= 0x2000
                fcntl.ioctl(fd, 0x541F, buf, False)
            except Exception:
                pass
        except Exception:
            pass
    return ser


def get_available_serial_ports() -> List[str]:
    """Returns sorted list of unique canonical (realpath) USB/ACM serial port device nodes."""
    ports: List[str] = []
    seen_real: Set[str] = set()

    def _add(path_candidate: str):
        if not path_candidate or not os.path.exists(path_candidate):
            return
        try:
            real_p = os.path.realpath(path_candidate)
        except Exception:
            real_p = path_candidate
        base = os.path.basename(real_p)
        # Skip onboard Pi SoC UARTs (/dev/ttyS0, /dev/ttyAMA0) when auto-scanning USB MCUs
        if base.startswith(("ttyS", "ttyAMA", "ttyprintk")):
            return
        if real_p not in seen_real and os.path.exists(real_p):
            seen_real.add(real_p)
            ports.append(real_p)

    if HAS_SERIAL:
        try:
            import serial.tools.list_ports
            for p in serial.tools.list_ports.comports():
                if p.device:
                    _add(p.device)
        except Exception:
            pass

    for pattern in ["/dev/ttyUSB*", "/dev/ttyACM*", "/dev/serial/by-id/*"]:
        for p in glob.glob(pattern):
            _add(p)

    return sorted(ports)


def probe_port_role(port: str, baud: int = UART_BAUD) -> Optional[str]:
    """
    Non-intrusively sniffs live binary frames on port (~10 ms typical) to identify ESP32 role
    using full XOR checksum validation before falling back to ASCII PING.
    """
    if not HAS_SERIAL or not os.path.exists(port):
        return None
    ser = None
    try:
        ser = open_low_latency_serial(port, baud)
        fd = ser.fileno()
        main_parser = BinaryStreamParser(is_tof_port=False)
        tof_parser = BinaryStreamParser(is_tof_port=True)
        accum = bytearray()

        # Phase 1: Passive binary/boot stream sniff (returns in ~10ms once streaming, up to 600ms on cold boot)
        t_deadline = time.perf_counter() + 0.600
        while time.perf_counter() < t_deadline:
            rem = max(0.001, t_deadline - time.perf_counter())
            rlist, _, _ = select.select([fd], [], [], rem)
            if rlist:
                chunk = os.read(fd, 4096)
                if chunk:
                    accum.extend(chunk)
                    main_parser.feed(chunk)
                    for msg_type, _ in main_parser.extract_packets():
                        if msg_type == MSG_ID_CHASSIS_TELEM:
                            return "main"
                    tof_parser.feed(chunk)
                    for msg_type, _ in tof_parser.extract_packets():
                        if msg_type in (MSG_ID_TOF_SUB_TELEM, MSG_ID_TOF_TELEM):
                            return "tof"
                    if (
                        b"PONG" in accum
                        or b"FOLKRACE" in accum
                        or b"STATUS REPORT" in accum
                        or b"[SPI Attempt" in accum
                        or b"STM32" in accum
                    ):
                        return "main"
                    if (
                        b"VL53L8CX" in accum
                        or b"[ToF " in accum
                        or b"Waking ToF" in accum
                        or b"RANGING" in accum
                    ):
                        return "tof"

        # Phase 2: Active ASCII ping fallback if stream was silent
        os.write(fd, b"PING\n")
        t_deadline = time.perf_counter() + 0.150
        while time.perf_counter() < t_deadline:
            rem = max(0.001, t_deadline - time.perf_counter())
            rlist, _, _ = select.select([fd], [], [], rem)
            if rlist:
                chunk = os.read(fd, 4096)
                if chunk:
                    accum.extend(chunk)
                    main_parser.feed(chunk)
                    for msg_type, _ in main_parser.extract_packets():
                        if msg_type == MSG_ID_CHASSIS_TELEM:
                            return "main"
                    tof_parser.feed(chunk)
                    for msg_type, _ in tof_parser.extract_packets():
                        if msg_type in (MSG_ID_TOF_SUB_TELEM, MSG_ID_TOF_TELEM):
                            return "tof"
                    if b"PONG" in accum or b"FOLKRACE" in accum or b"STATUS" in accum or b"STM32" in accum:
                        return "main"
                    if b"VL53L8CX" in accum or b"[ToF " in accum or b"Waking ToF" in accum or b"RANGING" in accum:
                        return "tof"
    except Exception:
        pass
    finally:
        if ser is not None:
            try:
                ser.close()
            except Exception:
                pass
    return None


def resolve_serial_ports(
    main_port: Optional[str] = "auto",
    tof_port: Optional[str] = "auto",
    baud: int = UART_BAUD
) -> Tuple[Optional[str], Optional[str]]:
    """Auto-detects and binds USB / UART serial ports to ESP32 Main and ESP32 ToF."""
    candidates = get_available_serial_ports()

    resolved_main = main_port if main_port and main_port != "auto" and os.path.exists(main_port) else None
    resolved_tof = tof_port if tof_port and tof_port != "auto" and os.path.exists(tof_port) else None

    # Filter out already explicitly claimed ports
    available = [c for c in candidates if c not in (resolved_main, resolved_tof) and os.path.exists(c)]

    # If both need detection, probe each available USB port
    if (resolved_main is None or resolved_tof is None) and available:
        probed_roles = {}
        for p in available:
            role = probe_port_role(p, baud)
            if role:
                probed_roles[p] = role

        # Assign based on probe findings
        for p, role in probed_roles.items():
            if role == "main" and resolved_main is None:
                resolved_main = p
            elif role == "tof" and resolved_tof is None:
                resolved_tof = p

        # Fallback for remaining unprobed or unassigned ports
        remaining = [p for p in available if p not in (resolved_main, resolved_tof)]
        if resolved_main is None and remaining:
            resolved_main = remaining.pop(0)
        if resolved_tof is None and remaining:
            resolved_tof = remaining.pop(0)

    return resolved_main, resolved_tof


class MCU_UART_Bridge:
    def __init__(
        self,
        main_port: Optional[str] = "auto",
        tof_port: Optional[str] = "auto",
        baud: int = UART_BAUD,
        udp_host: str = UDP_DISPATCH_HOST,
        udp_port: int = UDP_DISPATCH_PORT,
    ):
        self.main_port_arg = main_port
        self.tof_port_arg = tof_port
        self.baud = baud
        self.udp_host = udp_host
        self.udp_target = (udp_host, udp_port)
        self.running = False

        self.assigned_ports: Dict[str, Optional[str]] = {"ESP32_Main": None, "ESP32_ToF": None}
        self.last_known_ports: Dict[str, Optional[str]] = {"main": None, "tof": None}
        self.link_active: Dict[str, bool] = {"ESP32_Main": False, "ESP32_ToF": False}
        self.last_rx_timestamp: Dict[str, float] = {"ESP32_Main": 0.0, "ESP32_ToF": 0.0}
        self.force_reconnect_flag = False
        self.reconnect_gen = 0  # bumped per reset request so BOTH links always see it
        self.tof_reconnect_gen = 0  # bumped to reboot ONLY ESP32_ToF without resetting ESP32_Main
        self._last_tof_reboot_ts = 0.0
        self.discovery_lock = threading.Lock()

        self.udp_sock = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_DGRAM)
        try:
            self.udp_sock.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_SNDBUF, 524288)
        except Exception:
            pass
        self.udp_sock.setblocking(False)

        self.serial_lock = threading.Lock()
        self.main_serial: Optional[Any] = None
        self.tof_serial: Optional[Any] = None
        self.latest_mcu_msg: str = ""
        self.mcu_log: list = []

        # State aggregation & Option 1 Latency Compensator
        self.compensator = ToFLatencyCompensator()
        self.lock = threading.Lock()
        self.latest_chassis: Dict[str, Any] = {}
        self.latest_esp_servo_center_us: Optional[float] = None
        self.latest_esp_servo_trim_us: Optional[float] = None
        self.latest_tof_ranges = [0.0] * 16
        self.latest_tof_ranges_raw = [0.0] * 16
        self.latest_tof_target0_mm = [0] * 16
        self.latest_tof_target1_mm = [0] * 16
        self.latest_tof_target0_status = [255] * 16
        self.latest_tof_target1_status = [255] * 16
        self.last_tof_sensor_rx: Dict[int, float] = {sid: 0.0 for sid in SENSOR_CHANNEL_SLOTS}
        self._last_debug_dispatch = 0.0

    def _is_link_fresh(self, label: str, now: Optional[float] = None) -> bool:
        """True only when valid packets have arrived within LINK_TIMEOUT_S (mirrors STM32Channel::isConnected)."""
        if not self.link_active.get(label, False):
            return False
        t_rx = self.last_rx_timestamp.get(label, 0.0)
        if t_rx <= 0.0:
            return False
        t_now = time.monotonic() if now is None else now
        return (t_now - t_rx) <= LINK_TIMEOUT_S

    def _are_front_tofs_fresh(self, now: Optional[float] = None) -> bool:
        """True only when ALL critical front ToF sensors (2 & 4) have reported within LINK_TIMEOUT_S."""
        t_now = time.monotonic() if now is None else now
        for sid in CRITICAL_TOF_SENSORS:
            t_rx = self.last_tof_sensor_rx.get(sid, 0.0)
            if t_rx <= 0.0 or (t_now - t_rx) > LINK_TIMEOUT_S:
                return False
        return True

    def trigger_tof_reboot(self, reason: str = "Front ToF failure") -> bool:
        """Pulses EN on ESP32_ToF only (with cooldown) without disrupting ESP32_Main."""
        now = time.monotonic()
        if (now - self._last_tof_reboot_ts) < TOF_REBOOT_COOLDOWN_S:
            return False
        self._last_tof_reboot_ts = now
        print(f"[MCU Bridge] Auto-rebooting ESP32_ToF ({reason})...")
        self.tof_reconnect_gen += 1
        return True

    def _clear_link_state(self, label: str):
        """Resets cached telemetry for a disconnected/timed-out MCU link (zero fake/stale data)."""
        with self.lock:
            if label == "ESP32_ToF":
                self.latest_tof_ranges = [0.0] * 16
                self.latest_tof_ranges_raw = [0.0] * 16
                self.latest_tof_target0_mm = [0] * 16
                self.latest_tof_target1_mm = [0] * 16
                self.latest_tof_target0_status = [255] * 16
                self.latest_tof_target1_status = [255] * 16
                for sid in self.last_tof_sensor_rx:
                    self.last_tof_sensor_rx[sid] = 0.0
            else:
                self.latest_chassis = {}
                self.latest_esp_servo_center_us = None
                self.latest_esp_servo_trim_us = None

    @property
    def main_port(self) -> Optional[str]:
        return self.assigned_ports.get("ESP32_Main")

    @property
    def tof_port(self) -> Optional[str]:
        return self.assigned_ports.get("ESP32_ToF")

    def trigger_reconnect(self):
        """Forces immediate re-probe and reconnect across all MCU serial links."""
        print("[MCU Bridge] Manual reconnect command received. Re-detecting all MCU links...")
        self.reconnect_gen += 1
        self.force_reconnect_flag = True
        def _clear():
            time.sleep(1.0)
            self.force_reconnect_flag = False
        threading.Thread(target=_clear, daemon=True).start()

    def find_matching_port(self, target_role: str, excluded_ports: Set[Optional[str]]) -> Optional[str]:
        """
        Dynamically searches available serial ports for a matching MCU role ('main' or 'tof').
        Excludes canonical realpath ports actively assigned to other workers.
        """
        excluded_real = set()
        for ep in excluded_ports:
            if ep:
                try:
                    excluded_real.add(os.path.realpath(ep))
                except Exception:
                    excluded_real.add(ep)

        candidates = get_available_serial_ports()
        available = [p for p in candidates if p and p not in excluded_real and os.path.exists(p)]
        if not available:
            return None

        # Prioritize last known good port for this role to minimize reconnection time
        preferred = self.last_known_ports.get(target_role)
        if preferred and preferred in available:
            available.remove(preferred)
            available.insert(0, preferred)

        # 1. Probe roles
        for p in available:
            role = probe_port_role(p, self.baud)
            if role:
                self.last_known_ports[role] = p
            if role == target_role:
                return p

        # 2. Check explicit configured arg if it exists and wasn't claimed
        configured = self.tof_port_arg if target_role == "tof" else self.main_port_arg
        if configured and configured != "auto":
            try:
                conf_real = os.path.realpath(configured)
            except Exception:
                conf_real = configured
            if conf_real in available:
                return conf_real

        return None

    def start(self):
        if not HAS_SERIAL:
            print("[MCU Bridge ERROR] pyserial is not installed on this system!")
            return

        self.running = True

        # Start Command Ingestion Worker (listens for uplink packets to forward to ESP32 Main)
        t_cmd = threading.Thread(target=self._command_listener_worker, name="MCUCommandWorker", daemon=True)
        t_cmd.start()

        # Start resilient supervisor workers for both ESP32 Main and ESP32 ToF Hub
        t_main = threading.Thread(target=self._mcu_worker, args=("ESP32_Main",), name="Worker_ESP32_Main", daemon=True)
        t_main.start()

        t_tof = threading.Thread(target=self._mcu_worker, args=("ESP32_ToF",), name="Worker_ESP32_ToF", daemon=True)
        t_tof.start()

    def stop(self):
        self.send_to_main_mcu(b"ESTOP\n")
        self.running = False

    def _command_listener_worker(self):
        """Listens on UDP 127.0.0.1:9877 for motion/motor commands from GUI or planner and writes to ESP32."""
        sock = socket.socket(socket.AF_INET, socket.SOCK_DGRAM)
        sock.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_REUSEADDR, 1)
        try:
            sock.setsockopt(socket.SOL_SOCKET, socket.SO_RCVBUF, 262144)
        except Exception:
            pass
        try:
            sock.bind((self.udp_host, UDP_CMD_PORT))
            print(f"[MCU Bridge] Uplink Command Listener active on {self.udp_host}:{UDP_CMD_PORT}")
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"[MCU Bridge ERROR] Cannot bind UDP command port {UDP_CMD_PORT}: {e}")
            return

        sock.setblocking(False)
        while self.running:
            try:
                rlist, _, _ = select.select([sock], [], [], 0.05)
                if not rlist:
                    continue

                latest_motion_cmd: Optional[bytes] = None
                # Drain socket so bursts of 60Hz binary motion commands coalesce to the freshest frame
                while True:
                    try:
                        data, _ = sock.recvfrom(2048)
                    except (BlockingIOError, socket.error):
                        break
                    if not data:
                        continue

                    # Fast-path for 13-byte binary MotionCommandPacket (0x55 0xAA)
                    if len(data) == CMD_SIZE and data[:2] == PI_SYNC:
                        latest_motion_cmd = data
                        continue

                    d_strip = data.strip()
                    if d_strip in (b"RECONNECT", b"RESET_PORTS", b"RESTART_BRIDGE"):
                        self.trigger_reconnect()
                        continue
                    try:
                        if d_strip.startswith(b"{"):
                            cmd_obj = json.loads(data.decode("utf-8", errors="ignore"))
                            if isinstance(cmd_obj, dict) and cmd_obj.get("action") in ("reconnect", "reset_ports"):
                                self.trigger_reconnect()
                                continue
                    except Exception:
                        pass

                    # Immediate dispatch for explicit ASCII/control commands
                    self.send_to_main_mcu(data)

                if latest_motion_cmd is not None:
                    # Safety interlock: never drive without a fresh ToF link AND both critical front ToFs
                    if not self._is_link_fresh("ESP32_ToF") or not self._are_front_tofs_fresh():
                        latest_motion_cmd = encode_motion_command(0.0, 0.0, 0, 0)
                    self.send_to_main_mcu(latest_motion_cmd)
            except Exception:
                pass
        try:
            sock.close()
        except Exception:
            pass

    def send_to_tof_mcu(self, data: bytes):
        """Thread-safe transmission to ESP32 ToF Hub over USB Serial."""
        with self.serial_lock:
            if self.tof_serial and getattr(self.tof_serial, "is_open", False):
                try:
                    os.write(self.tof_serial.fileno(), data)
                except Exception as e:
                    print(f"[MCU Bridge ERROR] Failed writing to ESP32 ToF: {e}")

    def send_to_main_mcu(self, data: bytes):
        """Thread-safe zero-blocking transmission to ESP32 Main over USB Serial."""
        is_binary_motion = (len(data) == CMD_SIZE and data[:2] == PI_SYNC)
        with self.serial_lock:
            if self.main_serial and getattr(self.main_serial, "is_open", False):
                try:
                    os.write(self.main_serial.fileno(), data)
                    if not is_binary_motion:
                        preview = data.decode("utf-8", errors="replace").strip()
                        print(f"[MCU Bridge -> ESP32] Sent: {preview}")
                except Exception as e:
                    if not is_binary_motion:
                        print(f"[MCU Bridge ERROR] Failed writing to ESP32: {e}")
            elif not is_binary_motion:
                print(f"[MCU Bridge WARN] Cannot dispatch command (ESP32 serial offline)")

    def _mcu_worker(self, label: str):
        """
        Self-healing low-latency worker for an MCU connection.
        Uses event-driven select() + os.read() for <0.2ms packet ingestion latency
        (zero 256-byte buffering lag), detects disconnects, and reconnects in 250ms.
        """
        is_tof = (label == "ESP32_ToF")
        role_target = "tof" if is_tof else "main"
        configured_port = self.tof_port_arg if is_tof else self.main_port_arg

        while self.running:
            # 1. Port Acquisition Phase
            port = None
            if configured_port and configured_port != "auto" and os.path.exists(configured_port):
                try:
                    conf_real = os.path.realpath(configured_port)
                except Exception:
                    conf_real = configured_port
                with self.discovery_lock:
                    other_label = "ESP32_ToF" if label == "ESP32_Main" else "ESP32_Main"
                    if conf_real != self.assigned_ports.get(other_label):
                        port = conf_real
            else:
                with self.discovery_lock:
                    other_label = "ESP32_ToF" if label == "ESP32_Main" else "ESP32_Main"
                    excluded = {self.assigned_ports.get(other_label)}
                    port = self.find_matching_port(role_target, excluded)

            if not port:
                self.link_active[label] = False
                self.assigned_ports[label] = None
                time.sleep(0.5)
                continue

            self.assigned_ports[label] = port
            self.last_known_ports[role_target] = port
            print(f"[MCU Bridge] Connecting to {label} on {port} @ {self.baud} baud...")

            # 2. Connection and Low-Latency Event-Driven Streaming Phase
            parser = BinaryStreamParser(is_tof_port=is_tof)
            ser = None
            try:
                ser = open_low_latency_serial(port, self.baud)
                fd = ser.fileno()

                with self.serial_lock:
                    if is_tof:
                        self.tof_serial = ser
                    else:
                        self.main_serial = ser

                # Initialize MCU parameters without resetting or blocking
                try:
                    if is_tof:
                        os.write(fd, b"T\n")  # Pi multi-target mode: 44B (no SPAD)
                    else:
                        os.write(fd, b"STATUS\n")
                        os.write(fd, b"TRIM_GET\n")
