=== FILE: 3_esp32/ESP32_Main/pin_solver.py ===
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
pin_solver.py
=============
Master ESP32-S3 Mathematical Pinout Solver & Hardware Peripheral Verifier
Target: ESP32-S3-DevKitC-1 (N16R8 Octal Flash/PSRAM)
Base Reference: FOLKRACE Master Sensor & Actuator Hub

Peripherals:
  - 1x BNO086 9-DOF IMU over Dedicated SPI Master (SCK, MOSI, MISO, CS, INT, RST)
  - 1x Ackermann Steering Servo on LEDC PWM (333 Hz high-rate digital PWM)
  - 2x STM32G474 Motor Controllers on dedicated hardware UARTs:
      * STM32 #1 (Front Axle): Full Duplex (TX + RX @ 921,600 Baud)
      * STM32 #2 (Rear Axle): Full Duplex (TX + RX @ 921,600 Baud)
  - 1x Raspberry Pi 4 Host Telemetry Highway (Dedicated Hardware UART + DRDY interrupt pulse)
  - Native USB CDC Serial / JTAG preserved for flashing and debug monitoring

Priorities:
  1st Priority: Hardware peripheral correctness & conflict freedom (BNO086 SPI, Servo, 3x UART links, 0 collisions).
  2nd Priority: Physical spacing & EMI noise immunity (Header clustering, Clock-to-signal isolation, DC guards).
"""

import sys
import math
import argparse

# ==============================================================================
# 1. ESP32-S3 SILICON CONSTRAINTS & PHYSICAL HEADER MODEL
# ==============================================================================
ALL_SILICON_GPIOS = set(range(0, 49))
NON_EXISTENT_GPIOS = {22, 23, 24, 25}             # ESP32-S3 silicon skips GPIO 22-25
FORBIDDEN_OCTAL_FLASH_PSRAM = set(range(26, 38))  # GPIO 26-37: Internal Octal Flash/PSRAM (N16R8)
ONBOARD_WS2812_LED = {48}                         # Onboard RGB LED Data
INPUT_ONLY_GPIOS = {46}                           # GPIO 46 lacks output driver transistors
STRAPPING_GPIOS = {0, 3, 45, 46}                  # Bootloader strapping pins
XTAL32K_GPIOS = {15, 16}                          # Can be used as standard GPIO if 32k crystal absent
NATIVE_USB_GPIOS = {19, 20}                       # Dedicated Native USB D- / D+

# Physical 2D Pin Mapping on ESP32-S3 DevKitC-1: (side, row_1_to_22)
# Side: 'left' (Header J1), 'right' (Header J3)
PHYSICAL_GRID = {
    # Header J1 (Left Side)
    "3V3_1": ("left", 1), "3V3_2": ("left", 2), "EN": ("left", 3),
    4: ("left", 4), 5: ("left", 5), 6: ("left", 6), 7: ("left", 7),
    15: ("left", 8), 16: ("left", 9), 17: ("left", 10), 18: ("left", 11),
    8: ("left", 12), 3: ("left", 13), 46: ("left", 14),
    9: ("left", 15), 10: ("left", 16), 11: ("left", 17), 12: ("left", 18),
    13: ("left", 19), 14: ("left", 20), "5V": ("left", 21), "GND_J1": ("left", 22),

    # Header J3 (Right Side)
    "GND_J3_1": ("right", 1), 43: ("right", 2), 44: ("right", 3),
    1: ("right", 4), 2: ("right", 5), 42: ("right", 6), 41: ("right", 7),
    40: ("right", 8), 39: ("right", 9), 38: ("right", 10),
    37: ("right", 11), 36: ("right", 12), 35: ("right", 13),
    0: ("right", 14), 45: ("right", 15), 48: ("right", 16),
    47: ("right", 17), 21: ("right", 18), 20: ("right", 19), 19: ("right", 20),
    "GND_J3_21": ("right", 21), "GND_J3_22": ("right", 22),
}

# ==============================================================================
# 2. OPTIMIZED HARDWARE SIGNAL ALLOCATION
# ==============================================================================
ALLOCATION = {
    # BNO086 9-DOF IMU (Dedicated SPI Master) -- Header J1 Pins 15-20 (Adjacent to 5V & GND)
    "IMU_SPI_SCK":          {"gpio": 12, "dir": "OUT", "bus": "SPI2_HOST", "target": "BNO086 SCK (CLK)"},
    "IMU_SPI_MOSI":         {"gpio": 11, "dir": "OUT", "bus": "SPI2_HOST", "target": "BNO086 DI (MOSI)"},
    "IMU_SPI_MISO":         {"gpio": 13, "dir": "IN",  "bus": "SPI2_HOST", "target": "BNO086 SDO (MISO)"},
    "IMU_SPI_CS":           {"gpio": 10, "dir": "OUT", "bus": "GPIO",      "target": "BNO086 CSN (CS)"},
    "IMU_INT":              {"gpio": 9,  "dir": "IN",  "bus": "GPIO",      "target": "BNO086 HINTN (INT)"},
    "IMU_RST":              {"gpio": 14, "dir": "OUT", "bus": "GPIO",      "target": "BNO086 NRST (RST)"},

    # Single Ackermann Steering Servo (LEDC PWM) -- Header J3 Pin 18 (Directly adjacent to GND Pins 21-22)
    "SERVO_ACKERMANN_PWM":  {"gpio": 21, "dir": "OUT", "bus": "LEDC",      "target": "Steering Servo PWM (Signal)"},

    # STM32 Motor Controller 1 Link (Front Axle) -- Header J1 Pins 10-11 (Full Duplex)
    "STM32_1_UART_TX":      {"gpio": 17, "dir": "OUT", "bus": "UART1",     "target": "STM32 #1 PA10 (RX)"},
    "STM32_1_UART_RX":      {"gpio": 18, "dir": "IN",  "bus": "UART1",     "target": "STM32 #1 PA9 (TX)"},

    # STM32 Motor Controller 2 Link (Rear Axle) -- Header J1 Pins 8-9 (Full Duplex)
    "STM32_2_UART_TX":      {"gpio": 15, "dir": "OUT", "bus": "UART2",     "target": "STM32 #2 PA10 (RX)"},
    "STM32_2_UART_RX":      {"gpio": 16, "dir": "IN",  "bus": "UART2",     "target": "STM32 #2 PA9 (TX)"},

    # Raspberry Pi 4 Host Telemetry Link (Via Direct USB Cable to Pi 4 USB Port)
    # Onboard USB-to-UART bridge wires GPIO 43/44 internally to USB connector (0 header jumpers needed)
    "HOST_USB_TX":          {"gpio": 43, "dir": "OUT", "bus": "UART0",     "target": "Pi 4 USB Port (Internal USB-UART TXD0)"},
    "HOST_USB_RX":          {"gpio": 44, "dir": "IN",  "bus": "UART0",     "target": "Pi 4 USB Port (Internal USB-UART RXD0)"},
}

RESERVED_SYSTEM_PINS = {
    "USB_DM":      {"gpio": 19, "dir": "I/O", "bus": "USB_CDC", "target": "Native USB D-"},
    "USB_DP":      {"gpio": 20, "dir": "I/O", "bus": "USB_CDC", "target": "Native USB D+"},
    "BOOT_STRAP":  {"gpio": 0,  "dir": "IN",  "bus": "STRAP",   "target": "Onboard BOOT Button (GND when pressed)"},
    "JTAG_STRAP":  {"gpio": 3,  "dir": "IN",  "bus": "STRAP",   "target": "JTAG Selection Strap (Internal)"},
    "VDD_SPI":     {"gpio": 45, "dir": "IN",  "bus": "STRAP",   "target": "Flash Voltage Strap (Must be LOW)"},
    "ROM_PRINT":   {"gpio": 46, "dir": "IN",  "bus": "STRAP",   "target": "Boot ROM Print Strap (Input-Only)"},
    "WS2812_LED":  {"gpio": 48, "dir": "OUT", "bus": "RGB_LED", "target": "Onboard WS2812 RGB LED Data"},
}


# ==============================================================================
# 3. MATHEMATICAL PROOFS & HARDWARE VALIDATION
# ==============================================================================
def calc_pin_distance(g1, g2):
    """Calculates physical distance in 2.54 mm pin pitch units across board grid."""
    side1, r1 = PHYSICAL_GRID[g1]
    side2, r2 = PHYSICAL_GRID[g2]
    if side1 == side2:
        return float(abs(r1 - r2))
    # Distance between Left and Right headers: 7.0 pin pitches (17.78 mm) across MCU
    return math.sqrt(7.0**2 + (r1 - r2)**2)


def evaluate_hardware_and_metrics():
    assigned = {}
    violations = []

    for name, item in ALLOCATION.items():
        g = item["gpio"]
        direction = item["dir"]

        # Proof 1: Strict Injectivity (no pin collisions)
        if g in assigned:
            violations.append(f"COLLISION: GPIO {g} assigned to both '{assigned[g]}' and '{name}'")
        assigned[g] = name

        # Proof 2: Silicon Safety
        if g in NON_EXISTENT_GPIOS:
            violations.append(f"INVALID SILICON: GPIO {g} does not exist in ESP32-S3 silicon")
        if g in FORBIDDEN_OCTAL_FLASH_PSRAM:
            violations.append(f"FATAL CONFLICT: GPIO {g} interferes with internal Octal Flash/PSRAM")
        if g in ONBOARD_WS2812_LED:
            violations.append(f"RGB LED CONFLICT: GPIO {g} is wired to onboard WS2812")
        if g in STRAPPING_GPIOS:
            violations.append(f"BOOTLOADER CONFLICT: GPIO {g} interferes with boot strapping")
        if g in NATIVE_USB_GPIOS:
            violations.append(f"USB CONFLICT: GPIO {g} interferes with Native USB CDC")

        # Proof 3: Direction Validity
        if direction == "OUT" and g in INPUT_ONLY_GPIOS:
            violations.append(f"DIRECTION ERROR: GPIO {g} is input-only (GPI); cannot drive output '{name}'")

    if violations:
        print("ERROR: Hardware validation failed:")
        for v in violations:
            print("  *", v)
        sys.exit(1)

    # Proof 4: Physical Header Clustering & Spacing Metrics
    sck_gpio = ALLOCATION["IMU_SPI_SCK"]["gpio"]
    miso_gpio = ALLOCATION["IMU_SPI_MISO"]["gpio"]
    sck_miso_pitch = calc_pin_distance(sck_gpio, miso_gpio)

    # Distance between high dv/dt Servo PWM and sensitive SPI lines
    servo_gpio = ALLOCATION["SERVO_ACKERMANN_PWM"]["gpio"]
    min_servo_to_imu_pitch = min(calc_pin_distance(servo_gpio, item["gpio"]) for k, item in ALLOCATION.items() if "IMU_" in k)

    # Calculate free GPIO count
    free_gpios = sorted(list(
        ALL_SILICON_GPIOS
        - {item["gpio"] for item in ALLOCATION.values()}
        - {item["gpio"] for item in RESERVED_SYSTEM_PINS.values()}
        - FORBIDDEN_OCTAL_FLASH_PSRAM
        - NON_EXISTENT_GPIOS
    ))

    return {
        "total_assigned": len(assigned),
        "free_gpios": free_gpios,
        "sck_miso_pitch": sck_miso_pitch,
        "sck_miso_mm": sck_miso_pitch * 2.54,
        "servo_to_imu_pitch": min_servo_to_imu_pitch,
        "servo_to_imu_mm": min_servo_to_imu_pitch * 2.54,
    }


# ==============================================================================
# 4. STRUCTURED, COMPACT OUTPUT REPORT
# ==============================================================================
def display_results():
    metrics = evaluate_hardware_and_metrics()

    print("=" * 80)
    print("  ESP32-S3 MASTER SENSOR & MOTOR CONTROLLER PIN SOLVER REPORT")
    print("  Target: ESP32-S3-DevKitC-1 (N16R8 Octal Flash/PSRAM)")
    print("  Subsystems: BNO086 SPI IMU + Ackermann Servo + 2x STM32 UART + Pi 4 Telemetry")
    print("=" * 80)

    print("\n[MATHEMATICAL PROOF & HARDWARE VALIDATION]")
    print(f"  * Total Signals Assigned          : {metrics['total_assigned']} pins")
    print(f"  * Pin Collision Check             : PASSED (0 overlaps, strict 1:1 mapping)")
    print(f"  * Hardware UART Links             : CONFIRMED (UART0=Pi 4, UART1=STM32_1 Full-Duplex, UART2=STM32_2 Full-Duplex)")
    print(f"  * Dedicated SPI2 Master (BNO086)  : 6 Dedicated Pins (Adjacent to 5V/GND on Header J1)")
    print(f"  * Ackermann Steering Servo (LEDC) : Assigned to GPIO 21 (Header J3 Row 18)")
    print(f"  * Octal Flash/PSRAM (GPIO 26-37)  : SAFE (100% untouched)")
    print(f"  * Boot Strapping Pins (0,3,45,46) : SAFE (100% untouched)")
    print(f"  * Onboard RGB LED (GPIO 48)       : SAFE (100% untouched)")
    print(f"  * Native USB CDC (GPIO 19, 20)    : PRESERVED (Flashing & Interactive Serial Monitor)")

    print("\n[PHYSICAL CLUSTERING & EMI IMMUNITY METRICS]")
    print(f"  * IMU SPI Ribbon Pin Pitch        : Row 15 to Row 20 contiguous header block on J1")
    print(f"  * Servo PWM to IMU Separation     : {metrics['servo_to_imu_pitch']:.2f} pin pitches ({metrics['servo_to_imu_mm']:.2f} mm across board)")
    print(f"  * Dual STM32 Links Physical Block : Row 8 to Row 11 on Header J1 (adjacent comms)")
    print(f"  * Pi 4 Telemetry Connection       : Direct USB-C / micro-USB cable (0 header jumpers required)")

    def format_signal(sig, item):
        target = item.get("target", "")
        direction = item.get("dir", "")
        if not target:
            return sig
        if direction == "OUT":
            arrow = " -> "
        elif direction == "IN":
            arrow = " <- "
        else:
            arrow = " <-> "
        return f"{sig}{arrow}{target}"

    # Structured table with exactly SIGNAL and GPIO columns
    print("\n" + "-" * 72)
    print(f"  {'SIGNAL':<56} | {'GPIO':<9}")
    print("-" * 72)

    # BNO086 IMU
    print("  [BNO086 9-DOF IMU -- SPI2 DEDICATED MASTER]")
    for sig in ["IMU_SPI_SCK", "IMU_SPI_MOSI", "IMU_SPI_MISO", "IMU_SPI_CS", "IMU_INT", "IMU_RST"]:
        item = ALLOCATION[sig]
        sig_str = format_signal(sig, item)
        print(f"  {sig_str:<56} | GPIO {item['gpio']:<4}")

    # Ackermann Steering Servo
    print("\n  [ACKERMANN STEERING ACTUATOR -- LEDC HIGH-RATE PWM]")
    item = ALLOCATION["SERVO_ACKERMANN_PWM"]
    sig_str = format_signal("SERVO_ACKERMANN_PWM", item)
    print(f"  {sig_str:<56} | GPIO {item['gpio']:<4}")

    # STM32 Controller #1 Link
    print("\n  [STM32 MOTOR CONTROLLER #1 -- UART1 LINK (FRONT AXLE FULL-DUPLEX)]")
    for sig in ["STM32_1_UART_TX", "STM32_1_UART_RX"]:
        item = ALLOCATION[sig]
        sig_str = format_signal(sig, item)
        print(f"  {sig_str:<56} | GPIO {item['gpio']:<4}")

    # STM32 Controller #2 Link
    print("\n  [STM32 MOTOR CONTROLLER #2 -- UART2 LINK (REAR AXLE FULL-DUPLEX)]")
    for sig in ["STM32_2_UART_TX", "STM32_2_UART_RX"]:
        item = ALLOCATION[sig]
        sig_str = format_signal(sig, item)
        print(f"  {sig_str:<56} | GPIO {item['gpio']:<4}")

    # Raspberry Pi 4 Host Telemetry Link
    print("\n  [RASPBERRY PI 4 TELEMETRY LINK -- DIRECT USB CABLE (UART0/SERIAL)]")
    for sig in ["HOST_USB_TX", "HOST_USB_RX"]:
        item = ALLOCATION[sig]
        sig_str = format_signal(sig, item)
        print(f"  {sig_str:<56} | GPIO {item['gpio']:<4}")

    # System & Reserved
    print("\n  [SYSTEM RESERVED & HARDWARE DEBUG]")
    for sig, item in RESERVED_SYSTEM_PINS.items():
        sig_str = format_signal(sig, item)
        print(f"  {sig_str:<56} | GPIO {item['gpio']:<4}")

    print("-" * 72)
    print(f"  Available Free GPIOs ({len(metrics['free_gpios'])} pins): {metrics['free_gpios']}")
    print("=" * 80 + "\n")


def print_c_header():
    """Generates C/C++ Header `#define` configuration for config.h"""
    print("// =============================================================================")
    print("// AUTOGENERATED HARDWARE CONFIGURATION FROM pin_solver.py")
    print("// =============================================================================")
    print("// BNO086 9-DOF IMU (SPI2)")
    print(f"#define IMU_SPI_SCK_PIN          {ALLOCATION['IMU_SPI_SCK']['gpio']}")
    print(f"#define IMU_SPI_MOSI_PIN         {ALLOCATION['IMU_SPI_MOSI']['gpio']}")
    print(f"#define IMU_SPI_MISO_PIN         {ALLOCATION['IMU_SPI_MISO']['gpio']}")
    print(f"#define IMU_SPI_CS_PIN           {ALLOCATION['IMU_SPI_CS']['gpio']}")
    print(f"#define IMU_INT_PIN              {ALLOCATION['IMU_INT']['gpio']}")
    print(f"#define IMU_RST_PIN              {ALLOCATION['IMU_RST']['gpio']}")
    print()
    print("// Ackermann Steering Servo (LEDC PWM)")
    print(f"#define SERVO_STEER_PWM_PIN      {ALLOCATION['SERVO_ACKERMANN_PWM']['gpio']}")
    print(f"#define SERVO_ACKERMANN_PWM_PIN  SERVO_STEER_PWM_PIN")
    print()
    print("// STM32 Motor Controller 1 Link (UART1 - Front Axle Full Duplex)")
    print("#define STM32_1_UART_BAUD        921600")
    print(f"#define STM32_1_UART_TX_PIN      {ALLOCATION['STM32_1_UART_TX']['gpio']}")
    print(f"#define STM32_1_UART_RX_PIN      {ALLOCATION['STM32_1_UART_RX']['gpio']}")
    print()
    print("// STM32 Motor Controller 2 Link (UART2 - Rear Axle Full Duplex)")
    print("#define STM32_2_UART_BAUD        921600")
    print(f"#define STM32_2_UART_TX_PIN      {ALLOCATION['STM32_2_UART_TX']['gpio']}")
    print(f"#define STM32_2_UART_RX_PIN      {ALLOCATION['STM32_2_UART_RX']['gpio']}")
    print()
    print("// Raspberry Pi 4 Telemetry Link (Direct USB Cable @ 921,600 Baud)")
    print("#define PI_UART_BAUD             921600")
    print("// Zero header wires required; host telemetry links over onboard USB Serial (Serial)")
    print("// =============================================================================")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="ESP32-S3 Master Pinout Solver")
    parser.add_argument("--header", action="store_true", help="Print autogenerated C/C++ config header")
    args = parser.parse_args()

    if args.header:
        print_c_header()
    else:
        display_results()


=== FILE: 3_esp32/ESP32_Main/src/electronic_diff.cpp ===
#include "electronic_diff.h"

#ifndef PI_F
#define PI_F 3.14159265358979323846f
#endif

namespace {
// Yaw reference & gyro assist
constexpr float kGyroYawRefTauS = 0.055f;         // 55 ms chassis + servo yaw-rate buildup lag
constexpr float kGyroYawRefUnwindTauS = 0.020f;   // 20 ms fast unwind when straightening out
constexpr float kTvYawRefTauS = 0.012f;           // 12 ms short-lag uncapped yaw target for Rear TV
constexpr float kYawAccelLpfTauS = 0.00531f;      // ~30 Hz 1st-order LPF on clock-beat yaw accel
constexpr float kGyroDeadbandRadS = 0.28f;        // IMU yaw-rate noise deadband for servo countersteer
constexpr float kGyroKpSteer = 0.105f;            // Proportional gyro countersteer gain (rad / (rad/s))
constexpr float kGyroKdSteer = 0.004f;            // Derivative yaw damper gain (rad / (rad/s^2))
constexpr float kGyroKdRecoveryScale = 0.15f;     // Attenuate D-term during spin recovery so it won't unwind countersteer
constexpr float kGyroMaxRateRadS = 12.0f;         // Countersteer slew cap (rad/s) to reject landing/shock spikes
constexpr float kGyroFullSpeedMps = 0.60f;        // Speed where gyro assist reaches 100% authority

// Reactive slip telemetry latch timers (used for telemetry diagnostics)
constexpr float kUndersteerConfirmTimeS = 0.065f;
constexpr float kOversteerConfirmTimeS = 0.035f;
constexpr float kSlipHoldTimeS = 0.035f;

// Steering cap & asymmetric turn-in slew
constexpr float kSteerGripMargin = 1.25f;         // Extra grip margin on speed-based steer cap
constexpr float kPeakFrontSlipRad = 0.16f;        // ~9.2 deg peak front slip angle allowance
constexpr float kSteerSlewVLowMps = 1.20f;        // Speed below which turn-in uses full servo slew rate
constexpr float kSteerSlewVHighMps = 3.80f;       // Speed where turn-in slew reaches minimum taper
constexpr float kSteerSlewMinScale = 0.70f;       // Minimum turn-in servo slew scale at high speed (unwind stays 1.0x)

// Torque-envelope TCS, SVPWM headroom & Feedforward gains (GM3506 @ 12V, 5.6 Ohm)
constexpr float kVtrqFullV = 6.93f;               // Linear SVPWM phase-peak voltage headroom (12V / sqrt(3))
constexpr float kEmergencyCornerMu = 1.30f;       // High-mu emergency sanity ceiling (above Shore A22 grip)
constexpr float kEnvAccelMaxMps2 = 11.28f;        // ~1.15 g max longitudinal accel ramp
constexpr float kEnvDecelMaxMps2 = 11.28f;        // ~1.15 g max longitudinal braking ramp
constexpr float kFfAccelMaxMps2 = 7.50f;          // ~0.76 g accel FF scale ceiling (~4.58 V launch)
constexpr float kFfDecelMaxMps2 = 7.85f;          // ~0.80 g brake FF scale ceiling
constexpr float kFfTrackTauS = 0.12f;             // Velocity error -> longitudinal accel FF horizon
constexpr float kOverspeedFloorRadS = 18.0f;      // ~0.50 m/s safety overspeed floor above v_est
constexpr float kUnderspeedFloorRadS = 16.0f;     // ~0.44 m/s ABS underspeed floor below v_est
constexpr float kSlipCeilingRatio = 0.15f;        // 15% high-speed longitudinal slip allowance

#if TEMP_3WD_FR_DEAD
constexpr float kKaFront = 0.0f;                  // Zero front FF while FR is dead to prevent asymmetric torque steer
constexpr float kKaRear  = 0.72f;                 // Compensate rear FF in temporary 3WD mode
constexpr float kTemp3wdFlVtrqCap = 1.50f;        // Keep FL drive torque low until FR is restored
#else
constexpr float kKaFront = 0.61f;                 // 4WD launch FF gain (V / (m/s^2))
constexpr float kKaRear  = 0.61f;                 // 4WD launch FF gain (V / (m/s^2))
#endif

// Rear Torque Vectoring & Axle Envelope defaults
constexpr float kTvDeadbandRadS = 0.20f;          // +/- 0.20 rad/s deadband on yaw rate error
constexpr float kDefaultTvKpPsi = 4.0f;           // Rear TV proportional slip bias ((rad/s wheel) / (rad/s yaw))
constexpr float kDefaultTvKdPsi = 0.08f;          // Rear TV yaw acceleration damping ((rad/s wheel) / (rad/s^2))
constexpr float kDefaultTvMaxVff = 1.65f;         // Max rear TV feedforward torque voltage differential (V)
constexpr float kDefaultOversteerMuCap = 1.20f;   // Oversteer reference grip cap for Shore A22 silicone
constexpr float kTvSlipRatioMax = 0.10f;          // 10% wheel speed slip ceiling (8-12% Shore A22 peak)
constexpr float kTvSlipFloorRadS = 4.0f;          // 3-5 rad/s low-speed TV slip floor
constexpr float kFrontTurnMinFrac = 0.15f;        // Reserve ~85% front friction circle at full steering lock
} // namespace

ElectronicDifferential4WD::ElectronicDifferential4WD(
    float wheelbase,
    float track_rear,
    float track_front,
    float track_kingpin,
    float wheel_radius,
    float max_steer,
    float max_servo_rate,
    float ackermann_pct,
    float motor_dir,
    float v_max,
    float slip_gate,
    float thresh_spin
) : L_(wheelbase),
    W_r_(track_rear),
    W_f_(track_front),
    W_k_(track_kingpin),
    R_w_(wheel_radius),
    max_steer_rad_(max_steer),
    max_servo_rate_(max_servo_rate),
    ackermann_percent_(ackermann_pct),
    motor_dir_(motor_dir),
    v_max_wheel_(v_max),
    v_slip_gate_(slip_gate),
    thresh_spin_(thresh_spin),
    current_steer_rad_(0.0f),
    driver_steer_rad_(0.0f),
    prev_delta_gyro_rad_(0.0f),
    rate_limited_speed_(0.0f),
    omega_ref_(0.0f),
    omega_tv_target_(0.0f),
    prev_gyro_z_(0.0f),
    gyro_z_dot_filt_(0.0f),
    prev_gyro_seq_(0),
    prev_gyro_ts_us_(0),
    has_prev_gyro_(false),
    slip_state_(SLIP_NORMAL),
    slip_hold_timer_(0.0f),
    understeer_confirm_timer_(0.0f),
    oversteer_confirm_timer_(0.0f),
    gyro_assist_enabled_(true),
    rear_tv_enabled_(true),
    front_env_enabled_(true),
    tv_kp_psi_(kDefaultTvKpPsi),
    tv_kd_psi_(kDefaultTvKdPsi),
    tv_max_vff_(kDefaultTvMaxVff),
    mu_os_cap_(kDefaultOversteerMuCap) {}

void ElectronicDifferential4WD::reset() {
    current_steer_rad_ = 0.0f;
    driver_steer_rad_ = 0.0f;
    prev_delta_gyro_rad_ = 0.0f;
    rate_limited_speed_ = 0.0f;
    omega_ref_ = 0.0f;
    omega_tv_target_ = 0.0f;
    prev_gyro_z_ = 0.0f;
    gyro_z_dot_filt_ = 0.0f;
    prev_gyro_seq_ = 0;
    prev_gyro_ts_us_ = 0;
    has_prev_gyro_ = false;
    slip_state_ = SLIP_NORMAL;
    slip_hold_timer_ = 0.0f;
    understeer_confirm_timer_ = 0.0f;
    oversteer_confirm_timer_ = 0.0f;
}

ElectronicDiffOutputs IRAM_ATTR ElectronicDifferential4WD::update(
    float target_v,
    float target_steer_rad,
    float gyro_z_rad_s,
    float dt,
    float mu,
    float v_curr,
    uint32_t gyro_seq,
    uint64_t gyro_ts_us
) {
    ElectronicDiffOutputs out;
    const float safe_dt = (dt > 1e-5f) ? dt : CONTROL_LOOP_DT_S;
    const float safe_mu = fmaxf(0.10f, mu);

    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // 1. Driver Steering Intent (Speed-Capped & Asymmetric Slew)
    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    float target_delta = fmaxf(-max_steer_rad_, fminf(target_steer_rad, max_steer_rad_));
    float max_d_steer = max_servo_rate_ * safe_dt;

    float v_for_steer = fmaxf(fabsf(v_curr), fabsf(rate_limited_speed_));
    float steer_cap_rad = max_steer_rad_;
    if (v_for_steer > 0.50f) {
        float delta_grip = atanf((kSteerGripMargin * safe_mu * GRAVITY_G_CONST * L_) / (v_for_steer * v_for_steer));
        steer_cap_rad = fminf(max_steer_rad_, delta_grip + kPeakFrontSlipRad);
    }
    float capped_target_delta = fmaxf(-steer_cap_rad, fminf(target_delta, steer_cap_rad));

    bool is_steer_unwinding = (fabsf(capped_target_delta) < fabsf(driver_steer_rad_)) ||
                              (capped_target_delta * driver_steer_rad_ < 0.0f);
    float slew_scale = 1.0f;
    if (!is_steer_unwinding && v_for_steer > kSteerSlewVLowMps) {
        float t = fminf(1.0f, (v_for_steer - kSteerSlewVLowMps) / (kSteerSlewVHighMps - kSteerSlewVLowMps));
        slew_scale = 1.0f - t * (1.0f - kSteerSlewMinScale);
    }
    float max_d_driver = max_servo_rate_ * slew_scale * safe_dt;

    float driver_diff = capped_target_delta - driver_steer_rad_;
    if (fabsf(driver_diff) <= max_d_driver) {
        driver_steer_rad_ = capped_target_delta;
    } else {
        driver_steer_rad_ += copysignf(max_d_driver, driver_diff);
    }
    driver_steer_rad_ = fmaxf(-max_steer_rad_, fminf(driver_steer_rad_, max_steer_rad_));
    out.driver_steer_rad = driver_steer_rad_;
    out.driver_steer_deg = driver_steer_rad_ * (180.0f / PI_F);

    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // 2. Split Yaw References (Option C2) & Clock-Beat Guarded Yaw Accel
    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Always reference pre-countersteer driver_steer_rad_ and fused ground velocity
    float v_yaw = (fabsf(v_curr) > 0.05f) ? v_curr : rate_limited_speed_;
    float omega_kin_driver = (v_yaw * tanf(driver_steer_rad_)) / L_;

    // 2a. Short-lag (12 ms) uncapped kinematic yaw target for Rear TV turn-in
    float alpha_tv_ref = safe_dt / (kTvYawRefTauS + safe_dt);
    omega_tv_target_ += alpha_tv_ref * (omega_kin_driver - omega_tv_target_);
    out.omega_tv_target = omega_tv_target_;

    // 2b. Grip-capped (mu_os_cap_ ~ 1.20) & lagged reference for oversteer & gyro assist
    float omega_grip_max = (mu_os_cap_ * GRAVITY_G_CONST) / fmaxf(fabsf(v_yaw), 0.50f);
    float omega_ref_target = fmaxf(-omega_grip_max, fminf(omega_grip_max, omega_kin_driver));
    bool is_unwinding = (fabsf(omega_ref_target) < fabsf(omega_ref_)) || (omega_ref_target * omega_ref_ < 0.0f);
    float tau_ref = is_unwinding ? kGyroYawRefUnwindTauS : kGyroYawRefTauS;
    float alpha_ref = safe_dt / (tau_ref + safe_dt);
    omega_ref_ += alpha_ref * (omega_ref_target - omega_ref_);
    out.omega_ref = omega_ref_;

    // 2c. Clock-beat guard: differentiate gyro_z only on fresh BNO086 report with true inter-packet dt
    if (gyro_seq != 0U) {
        if (gyro_seq != prev_gyro_seq_) {
            if (has_prev_gyro_ && gyro_ts_us > prev_gyro_ts_us_) {
                float dt_gyro = (float)(gyro_ts_us - prev_gyro_ts_us_) * 1e-6f;
                if (dt_gyro <= 0.050f) {
                    float safe_dt_gyro = fmaxf(0.0015f, dt_gyro);
                    float d_gyro_raw = (gyro_z_rad_s - prev_gyro_z_) / safe_dt_gyro;
                    float alpha_d = safe_dt_gyro / (kYawAccelLpfTauS + safe_dt_gyro);
                    gyro_z_dot_filt_ += alpha_d * (d_gyro_raw - gyro_z_dot_filt_);
                }
            }
            prev_gyro_z_ = gyro_z_rad_s;
            prev_gyro_seq_ = gyro_seq;
            prev_gyro_ts_us_ = gyro_ts_us;
            has_prev_gyro_ = true;
        }
    } else {
        if (has_prev_gyro_) {
            float d_gyro_raw = (gyro_z_rad_s - prev_gyro_z_) / safe_dt;
            float alpha_d = safe_dt / (kYawAccelLpfTauS + safe_dt);
            gyro_z_dot_filt_ += alpha_d * (d_gyro_raw - gyro_z_dot_filt_);
        }
        prev_gyro_z_ = gyro_z_rad_s;
        has_prev_gyro_ = true;
    }
    out.yaw_accel_filt = gyro_z_dot_filt_;

    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // 3. Coordinated Continuous PD Gyro-Assisted Steering (Option B1)
    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // Strictly zero throughout normal turn-in / understeer [0 .. omega_ref_]
    float excess_yaw = 0.0f;
    if (omega_ref_ > 1e-4f) {
        if (gyro_z_rad_s > omega_ref_) {
            excess_yaw = gyro_z_rad_s - omega_ref_;
        } else if (gyro_z_rad_s < 0.0f) {
            excess_yaw = gyro_z_rad_s;
        }
    } else if (omega_ref_ < -1e-4f) {
        if (gyro_z_rad_s < omega_ref_) {
            excess_yaw = gyro_z_rad_s - omega_ref_;
        } else if (gyro_z_rad_s > 0.0f) {
            excess_yaw = gyro_z_rad_s;
        }
    } else {
        excess_yaw = gyro_z_rad_s;
    }

    float speed_abs = fabsf(v_yaw);
    float gyro_fade = 0.0f;
    if (speed_abs > v_slip_gate_) {
        gyro_fade = fminf(1.0f, (speed_abs - v_slip_gate_) / fmaxf(0.05f, kGyroFullSpeedMps - v_slip_gate_));
    }
    float speed_sign = (v_yaw >= 0.0f) ? 1.0f : -1.0f;

    float raw_delta_gyro = 0.0f;
    if (gyro_assist_enabled_ && gyro_fade > 0.0f && fabsf(excess_yaw) > kGyroDeadbandRadS) {
        float excess_db = copysignf(fabsf(excess_yaw) - kGyroDeadbandRadS, excess_yaw);
        float d_weight = fminf(1.0f, fabsf(excess_db) / 0.25f);
        float kd_scale = (excess_yaw * gyro_z_dot_filt_ >= 0.0f) ? 1.0f : kGyroKdRecoveryScale;
        float pd_raw = -(kGyroKpSteer * excess_db) - (d_weight * kd_scale * kGyroKdSteer * gyro_z_dot_filt_);
        if (excess_yaw > 0.0f) {
            pd_raw = fminf(0.0f, pd_raw);
        } else {
            pd_raw = fmaxf(0.0f, pd_raw);
        }
        raw_delta_gyro = gyro_fade * speed_sign * pd_raw;
    }

    float max_d_gyro = kGyroMaxRateRadS * safe_dt;
    float gyro_diff = raw_delta_gyro - prev_delta_gyro_rad_;
    if (fabsf(gyro_diff) <= max_d_gyro) {
        prev_delta_gyro_rad_ = raw_delta_gyro;
    } else {
        prev_delta_gyro_rad_ += copysignf(max_d_gyro, gyro_diff);
    }
    float delta_gyro = prev_delta_gyro_rad_;
    out.delta_gyro_rad = delta_gyro;

    // Physical servo dynamics (22.0 rad/s)
    float stabilized_target = fmaxf(-max_steer_rad_, fminf(driver_steer_rad_ + delta_gyro, max_steer_rad_));
    float steer_diff = stabilized_target - current_steer_rad_;
    if (fabsf(steer_diff) <= max_d_steer) {
        current_steer_rad_ = stabilized_target;
    } else {
        current_steer_rad_ += copysignf(max_d_steer, steer_diff);
    }
    current_steer_rad_ = fmaxf(-max_steer_rad_, fminf(current_steer_rad_, max_steer_rad_));

    out.steer_rad = current_steer_rad_;
    out.steer_deg = current_steer_rad_ * (180.0f / PI_F);

    float norm_steer = fmaxf(-1.0f, fminf(1.0f, current_steer_rad_ / fmaxf(1e-4f, max_steer_rad_)));
    if (norm_steer >= 0.0f) {
        out.servo_pulse_us = SERVO_PULSE_NEUTRAL_US - norm_steer * (SERVO_PULSE_NEUTRAL_US - SERVO_PULSE_MIN_US);
    } else {
        out.servo_pulse_us = SERVO_PULSE_NEUTRAL_US + (-norm_steer) * (SERVO_PULSE_MAX_US - SERVO_PULSE_NEUTRAL_US);
    }
    out.servo_pulse_us = fmaxf(SERVO_PULSE_MIN_US, fminf(SERVO_PULSE_MAX_US, out.servo_pulse_us));

    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // 4. Torque-Envelope Speed Target, Overspeed Ceiling & V_trq,ff (Option A1)
    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    float effective_target_v = target_v;
    // Emergency-only high-mu sanity ceiling (1.30 g); SL-MPC owns normal corner speeds
    float steer_for_cap = fmaxf(fabsf(driver_steer_rad_), fabsf(capped_target_delta));
    float tan_steer_cap = fabsf(tanf(steer_for_cap));
    if (tan_steer_cap > 0.05f) {
        float v_emerg = sqrtf((kEmergencyCornerMu * GRAVITY_G_CONST * L_) / tan_steer_cap);
        v_emerg = fmaxf(0.80f, v_emerg);
        if (effective_target_v > 0.0f) {
            effective_target_v = fminf(effective_target_v, v_emerg);
        } else if (effective_target_v < 0.0f) {
            effective_target_v = fmaxf(effective_target_v, -v_emerg);
        }
    }

    // Directional collision resync (never snap across zero away from effective_target_v)
    if (rate_limited_speed_ > (v_curr + 0.85f)) {
        rate_limited_speed_ = fmaxf(fminf(effective_target_v, 0.0f), v_curr);
    } else if (rate_limited_speed_ < (v_curr - 0.85f)) {
        rate_limited_speed_ = fminf(fmaxf(effective_target_v, 0.0f), v_curr);
    }

    if (effective_target_v <= v_curr) {
        if (rate_limited_speed_ > v_curr) {
            rate_limited_speed_ = fmaxf(effective_target_v, v_curr);
        }
    } else {
        if (rate_limited_speed_ < v_curr) {
            rate_limited_speed_ = fminf(effective_target_v, v_curr);
        }
    }

    float prev_v = rate_limited_speed_;
    float speed_err = effective_target_v - rate_limited_speed_;
    bool is_accel = (rate_limited_speed_ >= 0.0f && speed_err > 0.0f) || (rate_limited_speed_ <= 0.0f && speed_err < 0.0f);
    float max_dv = (is_accel ? kEnvAccelMaxMps2 : kEnvDecelMaxMps2) * safe_dt;
    if (fabsf(speed_err) <= max_dv) {
        rate_limited_speed_ = effective_target_v;
    } else {
        rate_limited_speed_ += copysignf(max_dv, speed_err);
    }

    // Reasonable overspeed / monotonic ABS underspeed envelope (~18 rad/s floor, 15% high-speed margin)
    float max_overspeed = fmaxf(kOverspeedFloorRadS * R_w_, kSlipCeilingRatio * fabsf(v_curr));
    float max_underspeed = fmaxf(kUnderspeedFloorRadS * R_w_, kSlipCeilingRatio * fabsf(v_curr));
    if (rate_limited_speed_ > 0.0f) {
        rate_limited_speed_ = fminf(rate_limited_speed_, fmaxf(0.0f, v_curr) + max_overspeed);
    } else if (rate_limited_speed_ < 0.0f) {
        rate_limited_speed_ = fmaxf(rate_limited_speed_, fminf(0.0f, v_curr) - max_overspeed);
    }

    if (v_curr > 0.30f && effective_target_v < v_curr) {
        float abs_floor = fminf(prev_v, v_curr - max_underspeed);
        rate_limited_speed_ = fmaxf(rate_limited_speed_, abs_floor);
    } else if (v_curr < -0.30f && effective_target_v > v_curr) {
        float abs_ceil = fmaxf(prev_v, v_curr + max_underspeed);
        rate_limited_speed_ = fminf(rate_limited_speed_, abs_ceil);
    }
    out.rate_limited_v = rate_limited_speed_;

    // Longitudinal acceleration feedforward (V_trq,ff = Ka * a_x_cmd)
    float v_ff_target = (rate_limited_speed_ < -0.05f && effective_target_v >= 0.0f)
                            ? rate_limited_speed_
                            : effective_target_v;
    float v_err_long = v_ff_target - v_curr;
    float a_x_cmd = 0.0f;
    if (fabsf(v_err_long) > 0.05f) {
        a_x_cmd = fmaxf(-kFfDecelMaxMps2, fminf(kFfAccelMaxMps2, v_err_long / kFfTrackTauS));
    }
    float v_ff_front_base = kKaFront * a_x_cmd;
    float v_ff_rear_base  = kKaRear  * a_x_cmd;

    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // 5. Kinematic Yaw Rate & 45% Geometric Ackermann Knuckle Headings
    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    float sin_d = sinf(current_steer_rad_);
    float cos_d = cosf(current_steer_rad_);
    float tan_d = tanf(current_steer_rad_);
    out.omega_cmd = (rate_limited_speed_ * tan_d) / L_;

    if (fabsf(tan_d) > 1e-4f) {
        float half_Wk_sin = (W_k_ * 0.5f) * sin_d;
        float L_cos = L_ * cos_d;
        float L_sin = L_ * sin_d;
        float steer_left_true = atan2f(L_sin, L_cos - half_Wk_sin);
        float steer_right_true = atan2f(L_sin, L_cos + half_Wk_sin);
        out.steer_left_rad = fmaxf(-VEHICLE_MAX_SERVO_RAD, fminf(VEHICLE_MAX_SERVO_RAD, current_steer_rad_ + ackermann_percent_ * (steer_left_true - current_steer_rad_)));
        out.steer_right_rad = fmaxf(-VEHICLE_MAX_SERVO_RAD, fminf(VEHICLE_MAX_SERVO_RAD, current_steer_rad_ + ackermann_percent_ * (steer_right_true - current_steer_rad_)));
    } else {
        out.steer_left_rad = current_steer_rad_;
        out.steer_right_rad = current_steer_rad_;
    }

    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // 6. Standstill Guard
    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    if (fabsf(rate_limited_speed_) < 0.001f && fabsf(v_curr) < 0.05f) {
        omega_ref_ = 0.0f;
        omega_tv_target_ = 0.0f;
        prev_delta_gyro_rad_ = 0.0f;
        slip_state_ = SLIP_NORMAL;
        slip_hold_timer_ = 0.0f;
        understeer_confirm_timer_ = 0.0f;
        oversteer_confirm_timer_ = 0.0f;
        out.omega_ref = 0.0f;
        out.omega_tv_target = 0.0f;
        out.delta_omega_tv = 0.0f;
        out.delta_vtrq_tv = 0.0f;
        out.v_FL = out.v_FR = out.v_RL = out.v_RR = 0.0f;
        out.omega_FL = out.omega_FR = out.omega_RL = out.omega_RR = 0.0f;
        out.rpm_FL = out.rpm_FR = out.rpm_RL = out.rpm_RR = 0.0f;
        out.vtrq_min_FL = out.vtrq_max_FL = out.vtrq_ff_FL = 0.0f;
        out.vtrq_min_FR = out.vtrq_max_FR = out.vtrq_ff_FR = 0.0f;
        out.vtrq_min_RL = out.vtrq_max_RL = out.vtrq_ff_RL = 0.0f;
        out.vtrq_min_RR = out.vtrq_max_RR = out.vtrq_ff_RR = 0.0f;
        out.scale_factor = 1.0f;
        out.slip_state = SLIP_NORMAL;
        out.yaw_error = 0.0f;
        out.is_countersteering = false;
        return out;
    }

    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // 7. Reverse Guard (Symmetric 4WD kinematic allocation, zero TV)
    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    if (effective_target_v < -0.05f || (rate_limited_speed_ < -0.05f && v_curr < 0.05f)) {
        out.yaw_error = gyro_z_rad_s - omega_ref_;
        out.slip_state = SLIP_NORMAL;
        out.is_countersteering = false;
        out.delta_omega_tv = 0.0f;
        out.delta_vtrq_tv = 0.0f;

        float half_Wr_omega = out.omega_cmd * (W_r_ * 0.5f);
        float half_Wf_omega = out.omega_cmd * (W_f_ * 0.5f);
        out.v_RL = rate_limited_speed_ - half_Wr_omega;
        out.v_RR = rate_limited_speed_ + half_Wr_omega;

        float fl_x = rate_limited_speed_ - half_Wf_omega;
        float fr_x = rate_limited_speed_ + half_Wf_omega;
        float front_y = L_ * out.omega_cmd;
        out.v_FL = fl_x * cosf(out.steer_left_rad) + front_y * sinf(out.steer_left_rad);
        out.v_FR = fr_x * cosf(out.steer_right_rad) + front_y * sinf(out.steer_right_rad);

        float v_peak = fmaxf(fmaxf(fabsf(out.v_FL), fabsf(out.v_FR)),
                             fmaxf(fabsf(out.v_RL), fabsf(out.v_RR)));
        out.scale_factor = 1.0f;
        if (v_peak > v_max_wheel_) {
            out.scale_factor = v_max_wheel_ / v_peak;
            out.v_FL *= out.scale_factor;
            out.v_FR *= out.scale_factor;
            out.v_RL *= out.scale_factor;
            out.v_RR *= out.scale_factor;
        }

        float max_omega = v_max_wheel_ / R_w_;
        out.omega_FL = fmaxf(-max_omega, fminf(max_omega, (out.v_FL / R_w_) * motor_dir_));
        out.omega_FR = fmaxf(-max_omega, fminf(max_omega, (out.v_FR / R_w_) * motor_dir_));
        out.omega_RL = fmaxf(-max_omega, fminf(max_omega, (out.v_RL / R_w_) * motor_dir_));
        out.omega_RR = fmaxf(-max_omega, fminf(max_omega, (out.v_RR / R_w_) * motor_dir_));

        const float rad_s_to_rpm = 60.0f / (2.0f * PI_F);
        out.rpm_FL = out.omega_FL * rad_s_to_rpm;
        out.rpm_FR = out.omega_FR * rad_s_to_rpm;
        out.rpm_RL = out.omega_RL * rad_s_to_rpm;
        out.rpm_RR = out.omega_RR * rad_s_to_rpm;

        out.vtrq_min_FL = out.vtrq_min_FR = -kVtrqFullV;
        out.vtrq_max_FL = out.vtrq_max_FR =  kVtrqFullV;
        out.vtrq_ff_FL  = out.vtrq_ff_FR  = fmaxf(-kVtrqFullV, fminf(kVtrqFullV, v_ff_front_base));
        out.vtrq_min_RL = out.vtrq_min_RR = -kVtrqFullV;
        out.vtrq_max_RL = out.vtrq_max_RR =  kVtrqFullV;
        out.vtrq_ff_RL  = out.vtrq_ff_RR  = fmaxf(-kVtrqFullV, fminf(kVtrqFullV, v_ff_rear_base));
        return out;
    }

    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // 8. Cornering State Classification (5-State Model + Hysteretic Telemetry)
    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    out.yaw_error = gyro_z_rad_s - omega_ref_;
    float os_frac = 0.0f;
    float us_frac = 0.0f;
    VehicleSlipState raw_slip = SLIP_NORMAL;

    if (speed_abs >= v_slip_gate_) {
        os_frac = fmaxf(0.0f, fminf(1.0f, (fabsf(excess_yaw) - 0.25f) / 0.95f));

        if (os_frac <= 0.05f && fabsf(driver_steer_rad_) > 0.08f && fabsf(omega_tv_target_) > 0.60f) {
            float turn_sign = (driver_steer_rad_ >= 0.0f) ? 1.0f : -1.0f;
            float us_deficit = (omega_tv_target_ - gyro_z_rad_s) * turn_sign;
            us_frac = fmaxf(0.0f, fminf(1.0f, (us_deficit - 0.20f) / 1.80f));
        }

        float dyn_thresh = fmaxf(fmaxf(thresh_spin_ * 2.2f, 0.95f), 0.40f * fabsf(omega_ref_));
        if (omega_ref_ > 0.10f && (gyro_z_rad_s - omega_ref_) > dyn_thresh) {
            raw_slip = SLIP_OVERSTEER;
        } else if (omega_ref_ < -0.10f && (omega_ref_ - gyro_z_rad_s) > dyn_thresh) {
            raw_slip = SLIP_OVERSTEER;
        } else if (fabsf(gyro_z_rad_s) > fmaxf(1.35f, thresh_spin_ * 3.0f) && (gyro_z_rad_s * omega_ref_ <= 0.0f)) {
            raw_slip = SLIP_OVERSTEER;
        } else if (us_frac > 0.45f) {
            raw_slip = SLIP_UNDERSTEER;
        }
    }

    if (speed_abs < v_slip_gate_) {
        slip_state_ = SLIP_NORMAL;
        slip_hold_timer_ = 0.0f;
        understeer_confirm_timer_ = 0.0f;
        oversteer_confirm_timer_ = 0.0f;
    } else if (raw_slip == SLIP_OVERSTEER) {
        oversteer_confirm_timer_ += safe_dt;
        understeer_confirm_timer_ = 0.0f;
        if (oversteer_confirm_timer_ >= kOversteerConfirmTimeS) {
            slip_state_ = SLIP_OVERSTEER;
            slip_hold_timer_ = kSlipHoldTimeS;
        }
    } else if (raw_slip == SLIP_UNDERSTEER) {
        understeer_confirm_timer_ += safe_dt;
        oversteer_confirm_timer_ = 0.0f;
        if (understeer_confirm_timer_ >= kUndersteerConfirmTimeS) {
            slip_state_ = SLIP_UNDERSTEER;
            slip_hold_timer_ = kSlipHoldTimeS;
        }
    } else {
        understeer_confirm_timer_ = 0.0f;
        oversteer_confirm_timer_ = 0.0f;
        if (slip_hold_timer_ > 0.0f) {
            slip_hold_timer_ = fmaxf(0.0f, slip_hold_timer_ - safe_dt);
        } else {
            slip_state_ = SLIP_NORMAL;
        }
    }
    out.slip_state = slip_state_;

    bool is_os = (os_frac > 0.05f);
    bool is_countersteering = is_os && ((current_steer_rad_ * gyro_z_rad_s) <= 0.03f * fabsf(gyro_z_rad_s));
    out.is_countersteering = is_countersteering;

    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // 9. Front Axle Envelope (Zero L/R Vectoring + Steering-Coupled V_trq)
    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    float steer_norm = fmaxf(0.0f, fminf(1.0f, fabsf(current_steer_rad_) / fmaxf(1e-4f, max_steer_rad_)));
    float front_steer_scale = kFrontTurnMinFrac + (1.0f - kFrontTurnMinFrac) * (1.0f - steer_norm) * (1.0f - steer_norm);
    bool is_braking = (effective_target_v < v_curr - 0.10f) && (v_curr > 0.20f);

    float v_f_max = front_env_enabled_ ? (kVtrqFullV * front_steer_scale) : kVtrqFullV;
    float v_f_min = is_braking ? (-kVtrqFullV * fmaxf(0.35f, front_steer_scale)) : 0.0f;
    float v_f_ff  = (a_x_cmd > 0.0f && front_env_enabled_) ? (v_ff_front_base * front_steer_scale) : v_ff_front_base;

    if (is_countersteering) {
        // Oversteer (countersteering): boost front V_trq_max + FF to drag nose straight
        v_f_max = kVtrqFullV * (0.65f + 0.35f * os_frac);
        v_f_min = 0.0f;
        v_f_ff  = fmaxf(v_ff_front_base, 1.5f + 2.5f * os_frac);
    } else if (is_os) {
        // Oversteer (still turned in): keep front low/neutral so pulling won't worsen spin
        v_f_max = fminf(v_f_max, 0.20f + 0.80f * (1.0f - os_frac));
        v_f_min = 0.0f;
        v_f_ff  = 0.0f;
    } else if (us_frac > 0.0f && front_env_enabled_) {
        // Understeer: drop front drive toward freewheel so 100% of front grip steers
        v_f_max *= (1.0f - 0.85f * us_frac);
        v_f_min = 0.0f;
        v_f_ff  *= (1.0f - us_frac);
    }

#if TEMP_3WD_FR_DEAD
    v_f_max = fminf(v_f_max, kTemp3wdFlVtrqCap);
#endif
    v_f_ff = fmaxf(v_f_min, fminf(v_f_max, v_f_ff));
    out.vtrq_min_FL = out.vtrq_min_FR = v_f_min;
    out.vtrq_max_FL = out.vtrq_max_FR = v_f_max;
    out.vtrq_ff_FL  = out.vtrq_ff_FR  = v_f_ff;

    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // 10. Rear Torque Vectoring (Bounded Slip Bias + Coordinated Yaw Damping)
    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    float delta_omega_tv = 0.0f;
    float delta_vtrq_tv = 0.0f;
    if (rear_tv_enabled_ && speed_abs >= v_slip_gate_) {
        float e_psi = is_os ? (-excess_yaw) : (omega_tv_target_ - gyro_z_rad_s);
        float e_db = 0.0f;
        if (fabsf(e_psi) > kTvDeadbandRadS) {
            e_db = copysignf(fabsf(e_psi) - kTvDeadbandRadS, e_psi);
        }

        // Option B1 guardrail: zero Rear TV Kd while delta_gyro servo is actively countersteering
        float tv_kd_scale = fmaxf(0.0f, 1.0f - fabsf(delta_gyro) / 0.06f);
        float delta_omega_max = fmaxf(kTvSlipFloorRadS, kTvSlipRatioMax * (speed_abs / R_w_));
        float d_term_tv = (fabsf(e_psi) > 0.15f || fabsf(gyro_z_dot_filt_) > 3.0f)
                              ? (tv_kd_scale * tv_kd_psi_ * gyro_z_dot_filt_)
                              : 0.0f;

        delta_omega_tv = fmaxf(-delta_omega_max, fminf(delta_omega_max, tv_kp_psi_ * e_db - d_term_tv));
        delta_vtrq_tv = (delta_omega_tv / fmaxf(1.0f, delta_omega_max)) * tv_max_vff_;
    }
    out.delta_omega_tv = delta_omega_tv;
    out.delta_vtrq_tv = delta_vtrq_tv;

    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // 11. Asymmetric Rear V_trq Bounds Across Cornering States
    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    float v_min_RL, v_max_RL, v_ff_RL;
    float v_min_RR, v_max_RR, v_ff_RR;

    if (is_os) {
        bool spin_left = (fabsf(gyro_z_rad_s) > 0.05f) ? (gyro_z_rad_s > 0.0f) : (excess_yaw > 0.0f);
        float outer_max = kVtrqFullV * fmaxf(0.0f, 1.0f - 1.4f * os_frac);
        float inner_max = is_countersteering
                              ? (kVtrqFullV * fmaxf(0.10f, 0.35f * (1.0f - 0.6f * os_frac)))
                              : (kVtrqFullV * 0.45f);

        if (spin_left) {
            // Left spin: RR is outer (cut V_trq -> 0); RL is inner
            v_min_RR = 0.0f;
            v_max_RR = outer_max;
            v_ff_RR  = 0.0f;

            v_min_RL = 0.0f;
            v_max_RL = inner_max;
            v_ff_RL  = is_countersteering ? 0.0f : fmaxf(0.0f, fminf(inner_max, v_ff_rear_base - delta_vtrq_tv));
        } else {
            // Right spin: RL is outer (cut V_trq -> 0); RR is inner
            v_min_RL = 0.0f;
            v_max_RL = outer_max;
            v_ff_RL  = 0.0f;

            v_min_RR = 0.0f;
            v_max_RR = inner_max;
            v_ff_RR  = is_countersteering ? 0.0f : fmaxf(0.0f, fminf(inner_max, v_ff_rear_base + delta_vtrq_tv));
        }
    } else {
        float lat_sig = v_yaw * gyro_z_rad_s + 0.35f * v_yaw * omega_tv_target_;
        float lat_load = fmaxf(0.0f, fminf(1.0f, fabsf(v_yaw * gyro_z_rad_s) / fmaxf(1.0f, safe_mu * GRAVITY_G_CONST)));

        float outer_max = kVtrqFullV;
        float outer_min = is_braking ? -kVtrqFullV : -1.0f;
        float inner_max = kVtrqFullV * (1.0f - 0.60f * lat_load);
        float inner_min = is_braking ? (-kVtrqFullV * (1.0f - 0.50f * lat_load)) : 0.0f;

        if (lat_sig >= 0.0f) {
            // Left turn: RR loaded outside, RL unloaded inside
            v_min_RL = inner_min;
            v_max_RL = inner_max;
            v_min_RR = outer_min;
            v_max_RR = outer_max;
        } else {
            // Right turn: RL loaded outside, RR unloaded inside
            v_min_RL = outer_min;
            v_max_RL = outer_max;
            v_min_RR = inner_min;
            v_max_RR = inner_max;
        }
        v_ff_RL = fmaxf(v_min_RL, fminf(v_max_RL, v_ff_rear_base - delta_vtrq_tv));
        v_ff_RR = fmaxf(v_min_RR, fminf(v_max_RR, v_ff_rear_base + delta_vtrq_tv));
    }

    out.vtrq_min_RL = v_min_RL;
    out.vtrq_max_RL = v_max_RL;
    out.vtrq_ff_RL  = v_ff_RL;
    out.vtrq_min_RR = v_min_RR;
    out.vtrq_max_RR = v_max_RR;
    out.vtrq_ff_RR  = v_ff_RR;

    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    // 12. 4-Wheel Speed Allocation + Bounded Rear TV Slip Bias
    // -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    float half_Wr_omega_rear = out.omega_cmd * (W_r_ * 0.5f);
    float half_Wf_omega = out.omega_cmd * (W_f_ * 0.5f);

    float v_tv_bias = delta_omega_tv * R_w_;
    out.v_RL = (rate_limited_speed_ - half_Wr_omega_rear) - v_tv_bias;
    out.v_RR = (rate_limited_speed_ + half_Wr_omega_rear) + v_tv_bias;

    // Front axle (zero L/R TV, vector projection along steered knuckle angles)
    float fl_x = rate_limited_speed_ - half_Wf_omega;
    float fr_x = rate_limited_speed_ + half_Wf_omega;
    float front_y = L_ * out.omega_cmd;
    out.v_FL = fl_x * cosf(out.steer_left_rad) + front_y * sinf(out.steer_left_rad);
    out.v_FR = fr_x * cosf(out.steer_right_rad) + front_y * sinf(out.steer_right_rad);

    // Proportional saturation scaling (preserve turning radius at speed limit)
    float v_peak = fmaxf(fmaxf(fabsf(out.v_FL), fabsf(out.v_FR)),
                         fmaxf(fabsf(out.v_RL), fabsf(out.v_RR)));
    out.scale_factor = 1.0f;
    if (v_peak > v_max_wheel_) {
        out.scale_factor = v_max_wheel_ / v_peak;
        out.v_FL *= out.scale_factor;
        out.v_FR *= out.scale_factor;
        out.v_RL *= out.scale_factor;
        out.v_RR *= out.scale_factor;
    }

    // Convert linear velocities to shaft angular velocities & RPM
    float max_omega = v_max_wheel_ / R_w_;
    out.omega_FL = fmaxf(-max_omega, fminf(max_omega, (out.v_FL / R_w_) * motor_dir_));
    out.omega_FR = fmaxf(-max_omega, fminf(max_omega, (out.v_FR / R_w_) * motor_dir_));
    out.omega_RL = fmaxf(-max_omega, fminf(max_omega, (out.v_RL / R_w_) * motor_dir_));
    out.omega_RR = fmaxf(-max_omega, fminf(max_omega, (out.v_RR / R_w_) * motor_dir_));

    const float rad_s_to_rpm = 60.0f / (2.0f * PI_F);
    out.rpm_FL = out.omega_FL * rad_s_to_rpm;
    out.rpm_FR = out.omega_FR * rad_s_to_rpm;
    out.rpm_RL = out.omega_RL * rad_s_to_rpm;
    out.rpm_RR = out.omega_RR * rad_s_to_rpm;

    return out;
}



=== FILE: 3_esp32/ESP32_Main/src/imu_bno08x.cpp ===
#include "imu_bno08x.h"
#include <cmath>
#include <esp_timer.h>

#ifndef M_PI
#define M_PI 3.14159265358979323846f
#endif

BNO08xIMU::BNO08xIMU() {
}

BNO08xIMU::~BNO08xIMU() {
}

bool BNO08xIMU::begin(int csPin, int misoPin, int mosiPin, int sckPin, int intPin, int rstPin) {
    Serial.println(F("\n========================================================"));
    Serial.println(F(" [BNO08x IMU] SparkFun Hardware SPI Driver Initialization"));
    Serial.println(F("========================================================"));
    Serial.printf(" [Config] CS=%d, INT=%d, RST=%d, SCK=%d, MISO=%d, MOSI=%d\n",
                  csPin, intPin, rstPin, sckPin, misoPin, mosiPin);

    // Initialize ESP32-S3 Hardware SPI bus
    SPI.begin(sckPin, misoPin, mosiPin, csPin);

    // Attempt 1: Standard Pin Assignment (CS=10, INT=9)
    Serial.printf(" [SPI Attempt 1/2] Connecting with CS=%d, INT=%d, RST=%d...\n", csPin, intPin, rstPin);
    if (bno08x_.beginSPI(csPin, 255, intPin, rstPin, 3000000, SPI)) {
        Serial.println(F(" [SUCCESS] SparkFun BNO08x Connected over SPI (CS=10, INT=9)!"));
        initialized_ = true;
        delay(100);
        setReportModeUs(REPORT_MODE_FUSION_GAME, DEFAULT_IMU_INTERVAL_US);
        return true;
    }

    // Attempt 2: Swapped Pin Assignment (CS=9, INT=10)
    Serial.printf(" [SPI Attempt 2/2] Swapping pins CS=%d, INT=%d, RST=%d...\n", intPin, csPin, rstPin);
    if (bno08x_.beginSPI(intPin, 255, csPin, rstPin, 3000000, SPI)) {
        Serial.println(F(" [SUCCESS] SparkFun BNO08x Connected over SPI (Swapped Pins CS=9, INT=10)!"));
        initialized_ = true;
        delay(100);
        setReportModeUs(REPORT_MODE_FUSION_GAME, DEFAULT_IMU_INTERVAL_US);
        return true;
    }

    Serial.println(F(" [ERROR] Unable to establish SPI link with BNO08x IMU over CS=10/INT=9 or CS=9/INT=10."));
    initialized_ = false;
    return false;
}

void BNO08xIMU::disableAllReports() {
    if (!initialized_) return;
}

void BNO08xIMU::setFeatureReportRateUs(uint8_t reportID, uint32_t intervalUs, uint32_t specificConfig) {
    bno08x_.shtpData[0] = SHTP_REPORT_SET_FEATURE_COMMAND;
    bno08x_.shtpData[1] = reportID;
    bno08x_.shtpData[2] = 0;
    bno08x_.shtpData[3] = 0;
    bno08x_.shtpData[4] = 0;
    bno08x_.shtpData[5] = (intervalUs >> 0) & 0xFF;
    bno08x_.shtpData[6] = (intervalUs >> 8) & 0xFF;
    bno08x_.shtpData[7] = (intervalUs >> 16) & 0xFF;
    bno08x_.shtpData[8] = (intervalUs >> 24) & 0xFF;
    bno08x_.shtpData[9] = 0;
    bno08x_.shtpData[10] = 0;
    bno08x_.shtpData[11] = 0;
    bno08x_.shtpData[12] = 0;
    bno08x_.shtpData[13] = (specificConfig >> 0) & 0xFF;
    bno08x_.shtpData[14] = (specificConfig >> 8) & 0xFF;
    bno08x_.shtpData[15] = (specificConfig >> 16) & 0xFF;
    bno08x_.shtpData[16] = (specificConfig >> 24) & 0xFF;
    bno08x_.sendPacket(CHANNEL_CONTROL, 17);
}

void BNO08xIMU::setReportMode(ReportMode mode, uint32_t intervalMs) {
    uint32_t clampedMs = (intervalMs == 0U) ? 1U : intervalMs;
    setReportModeUs(mode, clampedMs * 1000U);
}

void BNO08xIMU::setReportModeUs(ReportMode mode, uint32_t intervalUs) {
    if (!initialized_) return;

    if (intervalUs < 2000U) intervalUs = 2000U;
    current_mode_ = mode;
    report_interval_us_ = intervalUs;

    uint32_t rate_hz = 1000000U / intervalUs;
    uint32_t fusion_us = (intervalUs < 5000U) ? 5000U : intervalUs;   // Game RV / Gravity max 200 Hz to preserve SH-2 CPU headroom
    uint32_t aux_us = (intervalUs < 10000U) ? 10000U : intervalUs;     // Raw accel & mag capped at 100 Hz

    Serial.printf("[BNO08x IMU] Enabling Report Mode %d (Gyro/LinAcc: %u us / %u Hz, Fusion: %u us)...\n",
                  (int)mode, intervalUs, rate_hz, fusion_us);

    switch (mode) {
        case REPORT_MODE_FUSION_GAME:
            setFeatureReportRateUs(SENSOR_REPORTID_GYROSCOPE, intervalUs);
            setFeatureReportRateUs(SENSOR_REPORTID_LINEAR_ACCELERATION, intervalUs);
            setFeatureReportRateUs(SENSOR_REPORTID_GAME_ROTATION_VECTOR, fusion_us);
            setFeatureReportRateUs(SENSOR_REPORTID_ACCELEROMETER, aux_us);
            break;

        case REPORT_MODE_FUSION_9AXIS:
            setFeatureReportRateUs(SENSOR_REPORTID_GYROSCOPE, intervalUs);
            setFeatureReportRateUs(SENSOR_REPORTID_LINEAR_ACCELERATION, intervalUs);
            setFeatureReportRateUs(SENSOR_REPORTID_ROTATION_VECTOR, fusion_us);
            setFeatureReportRateUs(SENSOR_REPORTID_ACCELEROMETER, aux_us);
            setFeatureReportRateUs(SENSOR_REPORTID_MAGNETIC_FIELD, aux_us);
            break;

        case REPORT_MODE_FILTERED_IMU:
            setFeatureReportRateUs(SENSOR_REPORTID_GYROSCOPE, intervalUs);
            setFeatureReportRateUs(SENSOR_REPORTID_LINEAR_ACCELERATION, intervalUs);
            setFeatureReportRateUs(SENSOR_REPORTID_ACCELEROMETER, aux_us);
            setFeatureReportRateUs(SENSOR_REPORTID_GRAVITY, fusion_us);
            break;

        case REPORT_MODE_RAW_MEMS:
            setFeatureReportRateUs(SENSOR_REPORTID_UNCALIBRATED_GYRO, intervalUs);
            setFeatureReportRateUs(SENSOR_REPORTID_ACCELEROMETER, intervalUs);
            setFeatureReportRateUs(SENSOR_REPORTID_MAGNETIC_FIELD, aux_us);
            break;

        case REPORT_MODE_ALL:
            setFeatureReportRateUs(SENSOR_REPORTID_GYROSCOPE, intervalUs);
            setFeatureReportRateUs(SENSOR_REPORTID_LINEAR_ACCELERATION, intervalUs);
            setFeatureReportRateUs(SENSOR_REPORTID_GAME_ROTATION_VECTOR, fusion_us);
            setFeatureReportRateUs(SENSOR_REPORTID_ROTATION_VECTOR, aux_us);
            setFeatureReportRateUs(SENSOR_REPORTID_ACCELEROMETER, aux_us);
            setFeatureReportRateUs(SENSOR_REPORTID_MAGNETIC_FIELD, aux_us);
            setFeatureReportRateUs(SENSOR_REPORTID_GRAVITY, aux_us);
            break;
    }
}

// Rotate a 3D vector from IMU sensor frame to Robot Body frame (+X Forward, +Y Left, +Z Up).
// Physical mount: IMU +X points Left (+Y_body), IMU +Y points Back (-X_body), IMU +Z points Up (+Z_body).
static inline void rotateImuToBody(float ix, float iy, float iz, float &bx, float &by, float &bz) {
    bx = -iy;
    by = ix;
    bz = iz;
}

bool BNO08xIMU::update() {
    if (!initialized_) return false;

    // Check if BNO08x experienced a reset event
    if (bno08x_.hasReset()) {
        Serial.println(F("[BNO08x IMU] WARNING: BNO08x Sensor Was Reset! Re-enabling reports..."));
        delay(100);
        setReportModeUs(current_mode_, report_interval_us_);
    }

    bool got_new_data = false;

    // Drain pending SHTP packets from SparkFun BNO08x driver (bounded to prevent Core 0 hang)
    uint16_t report_id = 0;
    int drained = 0;
    while (drained++ < 8 && (report_id = bno08x_.getReadings()) != 0) {
        got_new_data = true;
        data_.last_update_ms = millis();
        data_.updated = true;

        switch (report_id) {
            case SENSOR_REPORTID_ROTATION_VECTOR:
            case SENSOR_REPORTID_GAME_ROTATION_VECTOR:
            case SENSOR_REPORTID_AR_VR_STABILIZED_ROTATION_VECTOR:
            case SENSOR_REPORTID_AR_VR_STABILIZED_GAME_ROTATION_VECTOR: {
                float qw = bno08x_.getQuatReal();
                float qx_i = bno08x_.getQuatI();
                float qy_i = bno08x_.getQuatJ();
                float qz_i = bno08x_.getQuatK();
                float qx, qy, qz;
                rotateImuToBody(qx_i, qy_i, qz_i, qx, qy, qz);
                float qnorm = sqrtf(qw * qw + qx * qx + qy * qy + qz * qz);
                if (qnorm > 1e-6f) {
                    float inv = 1.0f / qnorm;
                    qw *= inv;
                    qx *= inv;
                    qy *= inv;
                    qz *= inv;
                }
                data_.quat_w = qw;
                data_.quat_x = qx;
                data_.quat_y = qy;
                data_.quat_z = qz;
                convertQuaternionToEuler(qw, qx, qy, qz, data_.roll_deg, data_.pitch_deg, data_.yaw_deg);
                data_.quat_accuracy = bno08x_.getQuatRadianAccuracy();
                break;
            }

            case SENSOR_REPORTID_ACCELEROMETER:
                rotateImuToBody(bno08x_.getAccelX(), bno08x_.getAccelY(), bno08x_.getAccelZ(),
                                data_.accel_x, data_.accel_y, data_.accel_z);
                break;

            case SENSOR_REPORTID_GYROSCOPE:
                rotateImuToBody(bno08x_.getGyroX(), bno08x_.getGyroY(), bno08x_.getGyroZ(),
                                data_.gyro_x, data_.gyro_y, data_.gyro_z);
                data_.gyro_seq++;
                data_.gyro_timestamp_us = (uint64_t)esp_timer_get_time();
                break;

            case SENSOR_REPORTID_UNCALIBRATED_GYRO:
                rotateImuToBody(bno08x_.getUncalibratedGyroX(), bno08x_.getUncalibratedGyroY(), bno08x_.getUncalibratedGyroZ(),
                                data_.gyro_x, data_.gyro_y, data_.gyro_z);
                data_.gyro_seq++;
                data_.gyro_timestamp_us = (uint64_t)esp_timer_get_time();
                break;

            case SENSOR_REPORTID_MAGNETIC_FIELD:
                rotateImuToBody(bno08x_.getMagX(), bno08x_.getMagY(), bno08x_.getMagZ(),
                                data_.mag_x, data_.mag_y, data_.mag_z);
                break;

            case SENSOR_REPORTID_LINEAR_ACCELERATION:
                rotateImuToBody(bno08x_.getLinAccelX(), bno08x_.getLinAccelY(), bno08x_.getLinAccelZ(),
                                data_.lin_accel_x, data_.lin_accel_y, data_.lin_accel_z);
                data_.lin_accel_seq++;
                break;

            case SENSOR_REPORTID_GRAVITY:
                rotateImuToBody(bno08x_.getGravityX(), bno08x_.getGravityY(), bno08x_.getGravityZ(),
                                data_.gravity_x, data_.gravity_y, data_.gravity_z);
                break;

            default:
                break;
        }
    }

    return got_new_data;
}

void BNO08xIMU::convertQuaternionToEuler(float w, float x, float y, float z, float &roll, float &pitch, float &yaw) {
    float sinr_cosp = 2.0f * (w * x + y * z);
    float cosr_cosp = 1.0f - 2.0f * (x * x + y * y);
    roll = atan2(sinr_cosp, cosr_cosp) * (180.0f / M_PI);

    float sinp = 2.0f * (w * y - z * x);
    if (fabs(sinp) >= 1.0f)
        pitch = copysign(90.0f, sinp);
    else
        pitch = asin(sinp) * (180.0f / M_PI);

    float siny_cosp = 2.0f * (w * z + x * y);
    float cosy_cosp = 1.0f - 2.0f * (y * y + z * z);
    yaw = atan2(siny_cosp, cosy_cosp) * (180.0f / M_PI);
}

void BNO08xIMU::printMenu(Stream &out) {
    out.println(F("\n=============================================="));
    out.println(F("     BNO08x IMU Interactive Report Menu       "));
    out.println(F("=============================================="));
    out.println(F(" [1] Mode 1: 6-DOF Game Rotation Vector (Quaternion + Roll/Pitch/Yaw)"));
    out.println(F(" [2] Mode 2: 9-DOF Rotation Vector (Mag Integrated)"));
    out.println(F(" [3] Mode 3: Filtered IMU (Accel, Gyro, LinAccel, Gravity)"));
    out.println(F(" [4] Mode 4: Raw MEMS Counts (Raw Accel, Gyro, Mag)"));
    out.println(F(" [5] Mode 5: ALL Reports Active (Full Telemetry)"));
    out.println(F(" [+] Increase Rate (up to 400Hz)"));
    out.println(F(" [-] Decrease Rate (down to 50Hz)"));
    out.println(F(" [0] Reset Rate to Default (400Hz / 2500us)"));
    out.println(F(" [m] Display Menu"));
    out.println(F("==============================================\n"));
}

void BNO08xIMU::printCurrentReadings(Stream &out) {
    if (!initialized_) {
        out.println(F("[IMU] Hardware Not Initialized! Retry in progress..."));
        return;
    }

    switch (current_mode_) {
        case REPORT_MODE_FUSION_GAME:
        case REPORT_MODE_FUSION_9AXIS:
            out.printf("[FUSION %s] Quat (W:%.3f X:%.3f Y:%.3f Z:%.3f) | R: %6.2f°  P: %6.2f°  Y: %6.2f°\n",
                       (current_mode_ == REPORT_MODE_FUSION_GAME) ? "6DOF" : "9DOF",
                       data_.quat_w, data_.quat_x, data_.quat_y, data_.quat_z,
                       data_.roll_deg, data_.pitch_deg, data_.yaw_deg);
            break;

        case REPORT_MODE_FILTERED_IMU:
            out.printf("[FILTERED] Accel(m/s²): [%6.2f %6.2f %6.2f] | Gyro(rad/s): [%6.2f %6.2f %6.2f] | LinAcc: [%6.2f %6.2f %6.2f]\n",
                       data_.accel_x, data_.accel_y, data_.accel_z,
                       data_.gyro_x, data_.gyro_y, data_.gyro_z,
                       data_.lin_accel_x, data_.lin_accel_y, data_.lin_accel_z);
            break;

        case REPORT_MODE_RAW_MEMS:
            out.printf("[RAW MEMS] RawAccel: [%6d %6d %6d] | RawGyro: [%6d %6d %6d] | RawMag: [%6d %6d %6d]\n",
                       data_.raw_accel_x, data_.raw_accel_y, data_.raw_accel_z,
                       data_.raw_gyro_x, data_.raw_gyro_y, data_.raw_gyro_z,
                       data_.raw_mag_x, data_.raw_mag_y, data_.raw_mag_z);
            break;

        case REPORT_MODE_ALL:
            out.printf("[ALL TELEMETRY] R:%6.1f° P:%6.1f° Y:%6.1f° | Accel:[%.1f %.1f %.1f] | Gyro:[%.2f %.2f %.2f]\n",
                       data_.roll_deg, data_.pitch_deg, data_.yaw_deg,
                       data_.accel_x, data_.accel_y, data_.accel_z,
                       data_.gyro_x, data_.gyro_y, data_.gyro_z);
            break;
    }
}

void BNO08xIMU::handleSerialCommand(char cmd, Stream &out) {
    switch (cmd) {
        case '1':
            setReportModeUs(REPORT_MODE_FUSION_GAME, report_interval_us_);
            out.println(F("[BNO08x] Switched to Mode 1: 6-DOF Game Rotation Vector."));
            break;
        case '2':
            setReportModeUs(REPORT_MODE_FUSION_9AXIS, report_interval_us_);
            out.println(F("[BNO08x] Switched to Mode 2: 9-DOF Rotation Vector."));
            break;
        case '3':
            setReportModeUs(REPORT_MODE_FILTERED_IMU, report_interval_us_);
            out.println(F("[BNO08x] Switched to Mode 3: Filtered IMU (Accel/Gyro/LinAccel)."));
            break;
        case '4':
            setReportModeUs(REPORT_MODE_RAW_MEMS, report_interval_us_);
            out.println(F("[BNO08x] Switched to Mode 4: Raw MEMS Counts."));
            break;
        case '5':
            setReportModeUs(REPORT_MODE_ALL, report_interval_us_);
            out.println(F("[BNO08x] Switched to Mode 5: ALL Reports Active."));
            break;
        case 't':
        case 'T':
            tareAllAxes(out);
            break;
        case 'z':
        case 'Z':
            tareYawOnly(out);
            break;
        case 'p':
        case 'P':
            saveTare(out);
            break;
        case 'c':
        case 'C':
            clearTare(out);
            break;
        case 'r':
        case 'R':
            bno08x_.softReset();
            delay(150);
            setReportModeUs(current_mode_, report_interval_us_);
            out.println(F("[BNO08x] Soft Reset Executed & Reports Re-enabled."));
            break;
        case '+':
        case 'h': // Step up: 50Hz -> 100Hz -> 200Hz -> 400Hz
            if (report_interval_us_ > 10000U) {
                report_interval_us_ = 10000U;
            } else if (report_interval_us_ > 5000U) {
                report_interval_us_ = 5000U;
            } else {
                report_interval_us_ = DEFAULT_IMU_INTERVAL_US;
            }
            setReportModeUs(current_mode_, report_interval_us_);
            out.printf("[BNO08x] Rate updated to %u Hz (%u us).\n",
                       1000000U / report_interval_us_, report_interval_us_);
            break;
        case '-':
        case 'l': // Step down: 400Hz -> 200Hz -> 100Hz -> 50Hz
            if (report_interval_us_ < 5000U) {
                report_interval_us_ = 5000U;
            } else if (report_interval_us_ < 10000U) {
                report_interval_us_ = 10000U;
            } else {
                report_interval_us_ = 20000U;
            }
            setReportModeUs(current_mode_, report_interval_us_);
            out.printf("[BNO08x] Rate updated to %u Hz (%u us).\n",
                       1000000U / report_interval_us_, report_interval_us_);
            break;
        case '0': // Default (400Hz)
            report_interval_us_ = DEFAULT_IMU_INTERVAL_US;
            setReportModeUs(current_mode_, report_interval_us_);
            out.println(F("[BNO08x] Rate updated to 400Hz (2500 us)."));
            break;
        case 'm':
        case 'M':
            printMenu(out);
            break;
        default:
            break;
    }
}

void BNO08xIMU::tareAllAxes(Stream &out) {
    if (!initialized_) {
        out.println(F("[BNO08x] ERROR: IMU not initialized."));
        return;
    }
    uint8_t basis = (current_mode_ == REPORT_MODE_FUSION_9AXIS)
                        ? TARE_ROTATION_VECTOR
                        : TARE_GAME_ROTATION_VECTOR;
    bno08x_.tareNow(false, basis);
    out.println(F("[BNO08x] TARE_ALL COMPLETE: Roll, Pitch & Yaw set to 0."));
}

void BNO08xIMU::tareYawOnly(Stream &out) {
    if (!initialized_) {
        out.println(F("[BNO08x] ERROR: IMU not initialized."));
        return;
    }
    uint8_t basis = (current_mode_ == REPORT_MODE_FUSION_9AXIS)
                        ? TARE_ROTATION_VECTOR
                        : TARE_GAME_ROTATION_VECTOR;
    bno08x_.tareNow(true, basis);
    out.println(F("[BNO08x] TARE_YAW COMPLETE: Heading (Yaw) set to 0."));
}

void BNO08xIMU::saveTare(Stream &out) {
    if (!initialized_) {
        out.println(F("[BNO08x] ERROR: IMU not initialized."));
        return;
    }
    bno08x_.saveTare();
    out.println(F("[BNO08x] SAVE_TARE COMPLETE: Orientation persisted to BNO08x flash."));
}

void BNO08xIMU::clearTare(Stream &out) {
    if (!initialized_) {
        out.println(F("[BNO08x] ERROR: IMU not initialized."));
        return;
    }
    bno08x_.clearTare();
    out.println(F("[BNO08x] CLEAR_TARE COMPLETE: Persisted orientation reset to factory default."));
}
